We further introduce noise-adaptive memory control and scheduled Temporal State Consistency (TSC) to stabilize hidden representations across nearby noise levels. On OpenWebText, using a 32-step stochastic differential equation sampler, DeltaFlow-P reduces generated perplexity from 24.218 for the full-attention ELF baseline to 21.228 while maintaining comparable unigram entropy, with 36B training-token exposure compared with 45B for the baseline. In a denoiser-only benchmark, DeltaFlow-P achieves a 2.72x throughput speedup over the full-attention baseline at a sequence length of 16k. These results show that DeltaFlow is a promising alternative to dense attention for efficient continuous language denoising.

</details>


### [68] [RestoreKV: Recovering Full-Cache Behavior Under Aggressive Query-Agnostic KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01247)
*Changwoo Baek,Seungjun Shin,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RestoreKV 提出了一种新的KV缓存逐出范式：在保持原有逐出规则不变的前提下，用一个轻量级LoRA适配的"恢复令牌"（restore tokens）在预填充阶段从完整KV缓存中生成紧凑的上下文条件恢复缓存，以补充逐出后丢失的信息。该方法通过参数高效的自蒸馏从头到尾冻结的全缓存模型学习，仅优化0.4%的参数，无需任务特定微调，在多个骨干模型和长上下文基准上显著降低了压缩引起的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询无关KV缓存逐出方法在紧凑预算下性能会严重下降；此前的工作主要聚焦于改进保留哪些原始KV对的选择策略，而忽略了通过生成性/学习性手段补偿已丢失信息这一方向。

Method: 提出RestoreKV框架：1）在上下文预填充后，用少量"恢复令牌"（restore tokens）通过一次LoRA适配的pass关注完整KV缓存，生成紧凑且上下文条件的"恢复缓存"；2）基础重要性评分器和逐出规则保持不变，适配器在后续所有查询和解码阶段被禁用；3）通过参数高效的自蒸馏从头到尾冻结的全缓存模型进行训练，仅优化0.4%参数，无需任务特定调优。

Result: 在四个骨干模型和四个长上下文基准上，RestoreKV显著降低了压缩诱导的性能退化。在Qwen3-4B上，跨五种基础逐出方法、60个配对预算匹配设定中改善了59个；在5%预算下将KVzip从38.2提升到73.2（RULER-4K）；应用于KVzip+时在16倍压缩下达到86.4的RULER准确率（KVPress Benchmark），且仅增加不到0.5%的一次性缓存构建开销（32K上下文评估）。

Conclusion: RestoreKV证明选择型逐出与学习型恢复可以互补，利用跨上下文共享的恢复机制来弥补上下文特定的信息损失，是一种高效、通用的KV缓存压缩增强方案，具有极低的参数开销和运行时开销。

Abstract: Query-agnostic KV cache eviction compresses a context once and reuses the resulting cache for arbitrary future queries, but performance can collapse under tight budgets. Existing methods primarily improve which original KV pairs are retained. We introduce RestoreKV, which complements this selection-based formulation with learned restoration under the same total KV budget. Our key insight is that, although the information lost through eviction is context-specific, the mechanism for generating its compact complement can be shared across contexts. After context prefill, a few restore tokens attend to the full KV cache in a single LoRA-adapted pass, generating a compact, context-conditioned restore cache. The base importance scorer and eviction rule remain unchanged, and the adapters are disabled for all subsequent queries and decoding. RestoreKV is trained through parameter-efficient self-distillation from the frozen full-cache model, optimizing only $0.4\%$ of the parameters and requiring no task-specific tuning. Across four backbones and four long-context benchmarks, RestoreKV substantially reduces compression-induced degradation. On Qwen3-4B, it improves 59 of 60 paired, budget-matched settings across five base eviction methods; at a $5\%$ budget, it raises KVzip from $38.2$ to $73.2$ on RULER-4K. Applied to KVzip+, RestoreKV reaches $86.4$ RULER accuracy at $16\times$ compression on the KVPress Benchmark, while adding less than $0.5\%$ one-time cache-construction overhead in a 32K-context evaluation. Our project page is available at https://paper.pnu-cvsp.com/RestoreKV/

</details>


### [69] [ACE-GraphRAG: Agentic Context Engineering for Hierarchical GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01269)
*Yongfeng Huang,Yuren Lai,Ruiying Chen,Haoyu Huang,Mingming Zhao,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ACE-GraphRAG是一种面向层级GraphRAG的推理时上下文策略层，通过弥合"表示-推理鸿沟"来动态构建生成上下文，显著超越现有RAG和GraphRAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级GraphRAG以多粒度组织语料知识，但固定的上下文构建方式无法将这些多分辨率表示转化为适配当前查询的上下文，这种不匹配被定义为"表示-推理鸿沟"(representation-inference gap)。

Method: 提出Agentic Context Engineering (ACE-GraphRAG)，一种推理时的上下文策略层。其核心包括：(1) 将上下文构建形式化为一个基于gap感知细化的策略；(2) 并行差分检索(Parallel Differential Retrieval)从深度导向的事实分支和广度导向的语义分支获取补充证据，在保留来源与抽象层级的同时与初始上下文合并；(3) Full-ACE在任务族内统一应用完整策略，而Adaptive-ACE为单个查询选择任务和拓扑特定的策略。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和四个UltraDomain子集上的多跳QA和查询聚焦摘要任务中，Full-ACE优于所有RAG和GraphRAG基线；Adaptive-ACE进一步提升了多跳QA性能，并在全部四个UltraDomain子集上优于Full-ACE。

Conclusion: 消融实验和拓扑分析表明，上下文构建应被视为一个依赖于查询和任务的推理策略，而非固定的流程，这为层级GraphRAG的推理期上下文优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) organizes corpus knowledge at multiple levels of granularity, yet fixed context construction may fail to translate these multi-resolution representations into a context suited to the current query. We identify this mismatch as the representation--inference gap. We propose Agentic Context Engineering for Hierarchical GraphRAG (ACE-GraphRAG), an inference-time context policy layer that supplements and adapts the initial context for generation. ACE-GraphRAG formulates context construction as a policy over gap-aware refinement, retrieval branches, and task-conditioned adaptation. Parallel Differential Retrieval acquires supplementary evidence from depth-oriented factual and breadth-oriented semantic branches. These evidence increments are consolidated with the initial context while preserving provenance and abstraction levels. Full-ACE applies the full policy uniformly within each task family, whereas Adaptive-ACE selects task- and topology-specific policies for individual queries. We evaluate ACE-GraphRAG on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and four UltraDomain subsets across multi-hop QA and query-focused summarization. Full-ACE outperforms the evaluated RAG and GraphRAG baselines across both task families, while Adaptive-ACE further improves multi-hop QA and is preferred over Full-ACE on all four UltraDomain subsets. Ablation and topology analyses support treating context construction as a query- and task-dependent inference policy rather than a fixed procedure.

</details>


### [70] [ArabicDialectSafety: A Dialect-Aware Benchmark for Arabic Content Safety Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01291)
*Wajdi Zaghouani,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Kholoud Khalil Aldous,Mabrouka Bessghaier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了阿拉伯语方言安全数据集ArabicDialectSafety，包含25,071个提示，覆盖六种阿拉伯语变体（现代标准阿拉伯语、叙利亚、埃及、阿尔及利亚、巴勒斯坦和摩洛哥方言），并引入双任务评估框架用于二分类安全检测和细粒度危害分类的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语安全评估缺乏方言层面的细化研究，现有数据集多聚焦现代标准阿拉伯语，忽视了不同方言在安全评估中的差异和低资源马格里布方言的性能差距，需要构建多方言的安全评估基准。

Method: 构建人类策展的25,071条提示数据集，含六种阿拉伯语方言标签和七种细粒度危害类别标注；提出二分类（安全/不安全）与细粒度危害分类的双任务评估框架；对七种监督学习和生成式模型进行基准测试，并评估七个前沿LLM在不安全方言提示下的响应生成表现。

Result: 微调的MARBERTv2表现最佳，二元分类Macro-F1达0.95，细粒度分类达0.90，显著优于包括阿拉伯语专用模型在内的提示式前沿LLM。方言条件化在表示层面集成效果最佳，低资源马格里布方言仍存在显著性能差距。七个前沿LLM不安全生成率均低于5%。

Conclusion: 方言感知的阿拉伯语安全评估是可行且必要的，表示层面的方言条件化优于其他集成方式；低资源方言仍需进一步改进；数据集和代码将公开发布以支持后续研究。

Abstract: We present ArabicDialectSafety, a human-curated Arabic safety dataset of 25,071 prompts covering six Arabic varieties: Modern Standard Arabic, Syrian, Egyptian, Algerian, Palestinian, and Moroccan. The dataset is annotated with dialect labels and seven fine-grained harm categories. We introduce a dual-task evaluation framework for binary safe/unsafe detection and granular harm classification across dialects. Benchmarking seven supervised and generative models, we find that fine-tuned MARBERTv2 achieves the strongest performance, with Macro-F1 scores of 0.95 for binary classification and 0.90 for granular classification, substantially outperforming prompted frontier LLMs, including Arabic-specialized models. Our analyses show that dialect conditioning is most effective when integrated at the representation level, while significant performance gaps remain for low-resource Maghrebi dialects. We further evaluate seven frontier LLMs as response generators on harmful dialectal Arabic prompts and observe unsafe generation rates below 5 percent across models. We release the dataset and code upon acceptance to support future research on dialect-aware Arabic safety evaluation. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful and potentially offensive content included solely for research purposes.

</details>


### [71] [CrossLex: A Source-Grounded Benchmark for Cross-Jurisdictional Legal Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01292)
*Xiaocui Yang,Xican Tan,Shoujie Chen,Shihan Xiao,Keke Tong,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍CrossLex，一个基于权威法律来源、跨法域（中国、加州、德国）对齐的基准数据集，用于评估大语言模型在跨法域法律推理中的能力，包含6,149个实例、385个事实组和55个法律议题，并设计了三个互补任务及Grounded Joint评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律推理本质上依赖于法域（jurisdiction），相同事实在不同法律体系下可能适用不同规则并得出不同结论。然而现有基准很少评估LLM是否能识别这种法域特异性差异，尤其是在相同事实模式导致不同法律结果的情况下。作者希望填补这一空白，推动对LLM跨法域法律推理能力的系统评估。

Method: 作者构建了CrossLex基准：从中国、加州和德国的权威法律来源中提取55个法律议题（覆盖合同、消费者、刑事、家庭和劳动法），将相同事实模式对齐到三个法域，构造法域对齐的问题-答案-引注三元组。基准定义了三个互补任务：T1单法域推理、T2联合跨法域比较、T3细粒度跨法域评估。此外提出Grounded Joint指标，联合评估答案正确性和法律来源引用的准确性，并提供统一评估框架。

Result: 对代表性LLM的大量实验表明：当前模型通常能正确回答法律问题，但在提供准确的跨法域法律引注方面表现欠佳。

Conclusion: CrossLex有望促进源引用的跨法域法律推理研究，为后续工作提供标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Legal reasoning is inherently jurisdiction-dependent: the same facts can call for different legal rules and yield different conclusions across legal systems. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can recognize such jurisdiction-specific variation, especially when identical fact patterns lead to divergent legal outcomes.We introduce CrossLex, a same-fact, legal-source-grounded benchmark for evaluating cross-jurisdictional legal reasoning in LLMs across three jurisdictions: China, California, and Germany. Built from authoritative legal sources, CrossLex aligns 55 legal issues spanning contract, consumer, criminal, family, and labor law, and constructs jurisdiction-aligned questions paired with answers and supporting citations. In total, CrossLex contains 6,149 instances organized into 385 fact groups, with all legal issues, answers, and cited authorities reviewed by legal professionals.To disentangle basic legal knowledge from cross-jurisdictional reasoning, CrossLex defines three complementary tasks: single-jurisdiction reasoning (T1), joint cross-jurisdictional comparison (T2), and fine-grained cross-jurisdictional evaluation (T3). We further propose Grounded Joint, a metric that jointly assesses answer correctness and legal-source grounding, and provide a unified evaluation for streamlined benchmarking. Extensive experiments on representative LLMs show that, although current models can often answer legal questions correctly, they struggle to provide accurate cross-jurisdictional legal citations.We hope that CrossLex will facilitate future research on source-grounded cross-jurisdictional legal reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [RH-RAG: Trustworthy Long-Form Generation for Privacy-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01311)
*Raj Shekhar Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RH-RAG是一个多智能体框架，用于在本地部署的开放权重语言模型上实现安全可信的长文生成，通过Planner、Writer、Checker三个协同阶段以及双级检索索引，在保护数据隐私的前提下达到与专有云系统相当的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格隐私与安全约束下的组织难以使用专有云LLM API生成长篇内容；本地开放权重模型虽提供隐私保护替代方案，但现有RAG方法在较小模型上缺乏有效的全局规划，且长输出中容易积累事实不一致错误。

Method: RH-RAG将生成分解为三个阶段：(1) Planner Agent基于高层语义摘要构建全局文档大纲；(2) Writer Agent利用有界连贯性记忆逐节增量生成内容；(3) Checker Agent通过基于自然语言推理的事实验证和证明驱动的修订循环来缓解幻觉。框架还采用双级检索索引，支持在消费级硬件上进行高效规划和细粒度上下文生成。

Result: 在文学、金融和法律领域的评估表明，RH-RAG在事实接地性、语义连贯性和文档级对齐方面一致优于标准和层次化RAG基线，同时在不牺牲数据隐私的情况下达到与专有云系统竞争的可靠性。

Conclusion: RH-RAG证明了本地小模型通过多智能体协同架构可以有效弥补与大型云模型在长文生成能力上的差距，为隐私敏感组织提供了安全可信的本地长文生成方案。

Abstract: Generating long-form content from extensive internal reports remains challenging for organizations operating under strict privacy and security constraints, where proprietary cloud-based LLM APIs are often not viable. While locally deployed open-weight models offer a privacy-preserving alternative, existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches on smaller models frequently lack effective global planning and accumulate factual inconsistencies over long outputs. To address these limitations, we present RH-RAG, a multi-agent framework for secure and trustworthy long form generation using local language models. RH-RAG decomposes generation into three coordinated stages: a Planner Agent that constructs a global document outline from high-level semantic summaries, a Writer Agent that incrementally generates coherent section-wise content using bounded coherence memory, and a Checker Agent that mitigates hallucinations through natural language inference-based factual verification and an attestation-driven revision loop. The framework further employs a dual-level retrieval index that supports efficient planning and fine-grained contextual generation on consumer-grade hardware. Evaluations across literary, financial, and legal domains demonstrate that RH-RAG consistently improves factual grounding, semantic coherence, and document-level alignment compared to standard and hierarchical RAG baselines, while achieving reliability competitive with proprietary cloud-based systems without compromising data privacy.

</details>


### [73] [BiCAA: Bidirectional Credit Assignment for Search-Augmented Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01321)
*Yibin Huang,Bin Xu,Hailong Cao,Conghui Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCAA，一种用于多步搜索增强智能体的双向信用分配框架，通过融合前向可解性增益和后见成功关键性两种互补的过程奖励信号，为中间推理步骤提供密集的逐步监督，以解决vanilla GRPO中仅基于最终输出的稀疏奖励导致的训练不稳定和冗余搜索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步搜索是搜索智能体的基础能力，但vanilla GRPO仅基于最终输出分配奖励，缺乏对中间推理步骤的监督信号。这种稀疏的监督容易导致多步搜索任务中的训练不稳定和冗余搜索行为，因此需要引入过程奖励来提供逐步的监督信号。

Method: 提出两个互补的评判标准来评估每个搜索步骤：(1)该步骤是否产生有助于解决问题的新证据；(2)该步骤是否在整体推理轨迹中形成高效、关键的中间决策。基于此构建BiCAA双向信用分配框架，融合两个互补信号：前向可解性增益（量化回答可信度的逐步改进）和后见成功关键性（通过后见结果关键性评分评估每步对最终成功的必要性），调制聚合两个信号后与结果奖励融合。

Result: 在搜索增强QA基准上的实验表明，BiCAA能够稳定策略优化，减少冗余搜索行为，并达到有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: BiCAA通过提供密集、有区分度的过程奖励，有效解决了多步搜索任务中稀疏奖励监督导致的训练不稳定和冗余行为问题，为搜索增强智能体的训练提供了更有效的信用分配方案。

Abstract: Multi-step search is a fundamental capability for search agents, enabling them to iteratively acquire, refine, and integrate external evidence for complex reasoning QA. However, vanilla GRPO allocates rewards exclusively based on the model's final outputs, yielding outcome-only supervision with no supervisory signals for intermediate reasoning steps. Such sparse supervision easily causes training instability and redundant search behaviors on multi-step search tasks. To mitigate this limitation, we adopt process reward to deliver stepwise supervision signals. For this process reward, we propose two complementary criteria to judge each search step: whether the step yields new evidence to facilitate problem solving, and whether it forms an efficient, pivotal intermediate decision within the overall reasoning trajectory. Building on this insight, we propose BiCAA: a bidirectional credit assignment framework that delivers dense, distinguishing process rewards for search-augmented agents. BiCAA builds bidirectional process rewards by fusing two complementary signals: forward solvability gain and hindsight success criticality. The former quantifies step-wise improvements in answer plausibility, while the latter evaluates each step's necessity for final success via hindsight outcome-based criticality scoring. We modulate and aggregate the two signals and then fuse them with the outcome reward. Experiments on search-augmented QA benchmarks show that BiCAA stabilizes policy optimization, reduces redundant search behavior, and achieves competitive performance.

</details>


### [74] [Can Language Models Identify Shadow Trading Targets? An NLP Evaluation of SEC Enforcement Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01322)
*Sarah Wilson,Michael MacKay,Anthony Marello,Trinav Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验了NLP/LLM能否事前识别"影子交易"（shadow trading）中的经济关联公司。研究者用两阶段LLM流水线分析SEC 10-K文件中Item 7（MD&A）文本的语义相似度，并将其与30个并购事件的公告日异常收益关联。虽然在Panuwat案例本身（Incyte）上有所验证，但整体数据集显示无显著关联：秩相关+0.07（p=0.37），置信区间窄到足以排除中等强度的关系。研究对影子交易执法的经验前提提出了质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影子交易是内幕交易责任的一种新颖且有争议的理论，首次在SEC v. Panuwat (2023)中被起诉。SEC执行该理论需要事前识别经济关联公司，但其自身仅靠事后的大规模市场监控基础设施来判断。作者质疑：既然SEC理论预设内幕人士知道哪些公司是经济关联的，那么NLP能否从公开文件（10-K）中事前识别这些同行公司？这关系到影子交易执法乃至SEC金融监控基础设施的合宪性讨论。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM流水线，应用于SEC 10-K文件的Item 7（管理层讨论与分析，MD&A）部分。对30个跨五个行业的并购事件计算语义相似度，并将相似度与公告日异常股票收益进行关联分析。具体包括：对Panuwat案本身做反事实验证；汇总217个同行观察值计算within-event秩相关和per-event Spearman相关（含置换检验p值和95%置信区间）；按case层面分类（支持/矛盾/模糊）；并检查Incyte的市值波段归属。

Result: 在Panuwat案中，流水线成功将Incyte识别为最接近的同行之一（对该已知结果案例的合理性检验通过）。但全数据集显示无关联：within-event秩相关+0.07（置换p=0.37），per-event Spearman均值为+0.05，95%CI为[-0.08, +0.18]，该区间窄到足以排除中等强度关系。case层面30个事件中14个支持假设、12个矛盾、4个模糊。此外，Incyte在公告前一日市值不在标准$20亿-$100亿中盘股区间内，使SEC引用的"中盘肿瘤学"类别变得复杂。

Conclusion: 这些结果是探索性的，受限于该流水线、语料库和收益度量方式，但确实对影子交易执法的经验前提构成压力，并涉及SEC金融监控基础设施的合宪性问题。核心结论是：虽然NLP能恢复单一已知案例（Panuwat/Incyte）中的同行识别，但在更大规模数据上无法建立语义相似度与市场反应的系统性关联，削弱了"内幕人士从事前已知的经济关联中获得信息优势"这一执法理论的经验基础。

Abstract: Shadow trading -- trading in a peer firm's securities on the basis of material nonpublic information (MNPI) about an "economically linked" company -- is a novel and contested theory of insider trading liability, first prosecuted in SEC v. Panuwat (2023). Enforcing it requires identifying economically linked firms ex ante, a determination the SEC makes only after the fact using mass market surveillance infrastructure. We ask whether NLP can do what the SEC's theory presumes insiders already know: identify peer firms ex ante from publicly mandated disclosures. Using a two-stage LLM pipeline applied to Item 7 (Management's Discussion and Analysis) sections of SEC 10-K filings, we score semantic similarity across 30 M&A events spanning five industries and relate similarity to announcement-day abnormal stock returns. On the Panuwat fact pattern itself the pipeline recovers Incyte among the closest peers, a sanity check on the one case with a known outcome. Across the full dataset, however, we find no association: pooling 217 peer observations, the within-event rank correlation between similarity and abnormal return is +0.07 (permutation p = 0.37), and the mean per-event Spearman correlation is +0.05 with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.08, +0.18] -- narrow enough to exclude any moderate relationship rather than merely failing to detect one. A case-level reading agrees: 14 of 30 events support the hypothesis, 12 contradict it, and 4 are ambiguous. We also find that Incyte fell outside the standard \$2B-\$10B mid-cap band on the day before the announcement, complicating the "mid-cap oncology" category the SEC invoked. These results are exploratory and bound to this pipeline, corpus, and return measure, but they put pressure on the empirical premise of shadow trading enforcement and bear on constitutional questions surrounding the SEC's financial surveillance infrastructure.

</details>


### [75] [LongChart VQA: A Comprehensive Benchmark for MLLMs with Complex Multi-Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01328)
*Ziyan Xiao,Yinghao Zhu,Wenting Zhang,Heaju Kim,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LongChart基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在多图表理解和多步推理方面的能力。该基准通过基于潜在图的合成管道生成，平均包含6.5张图像和31.2个问题。作者评估了10个SOTA MLLM，发现随着计算复杂度增加，模型准确率下降且差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准大多强调单图表感知，简单的图表间连接不足以评估模型在多图表理解、多步推理和复杂代理任务中的能力。随着MLLM被更广泛地应用于复杂agentic任务，需要一个能够捕捉多图表复杂性同时保证一致性和有效性的评估基准。

Method: 设计了一个由潜在图（latent graphs）支撑的合成管道（synthesis pipeline）来生成基准数据，确保多图表场景的复杂性与数据的一致性、有效性。基于此管道构建了LongChart基准，其VQA集合平均包含6.5张图像和31.2个问题。评估了10个SOTA MLLM，考察推理模式、辅助工具和图像扰动鲁棒性三个影响因素。

Result: 实验结果表明，随着计算复杂度的增加，MLLM的准确率下降且变化显著，揭示了多图表推理场景中模型性能的不稳定性。

Conclusion: MLLMs在多图表推理方面仍存在明显不足，尤其是在高计算复杂度场景下性能退化明显，这为未来多图表推理研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving with expanded context windows and stronger reasoning capabilities, enabling multi-chart understanding and multi-step inference. These abilities are increasingly important as MLLMs are adopted in complex agentic tasks. However, existing benchmarks largely emphasize single-chart perception, while simple chart-to-chart connections are insufficient to evaluate these capabilities. To capture multi-chart complexity while ensuring consistency and validity, we design a synthesis pipeline supported by latent graphs. Building on this pipeline, we introduce LongChart, a benchmark whose VQA sets contain an average of 6.5 images and 31.2 questions. We evaluate 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs and examine three factors that influence performance: reasoning patterns, auxiliary tools, and robustness to image perturbations. Our results show that MLLM accuracy decreases and varies substantially as computational complexity increases, highlighting directions for future research in multi-chart reasoning.

</details>


### [76] [Prompt-Induced Waste in Large Reasoning Models: A Preregistered Two-Harness Benchmark of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01347)
*Sarel Weinberger,Amir Hozez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册基准测试，在六个大型推理模型、两个真实代理工具框架和24个确定性编码任务中系统测量了提示词表述对编码代理推理成本的影响。研究发现提示词表述可成倍增加推理成本而不提升正确性，其中要求模型开发并比较多种方法是最浪费的指令（推理token增加2.4-7.4倍），"深入思考"类提示增加1.6-2.2倍，而带有范围、验收标准和停止条件的边界效率模板成本中性且可将推理减半。此外，工具框架选择的影响更大（相同任务在Claude Code下的每成功成本比pi高5-30倍），误导性架构提示比无关文本代价更高，提供商端缓存减少了计费成本但未改变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型作为编码代理会产生源自推理、工具调用和重复代理轮次的显著成本，但提示词表述对此类支出的因果影响尚未被系统量化。作者旨在通过严格基准测试填补这一空白，为提示工程和代理设计提供基于证据的成本优化指导。

Method: 采用预注册基准测试设计，涵盖六个大型推理模型、两个真实代理工具框架（Claude Code和pi）、24个确定性编码任务（含隐藏评估器）。共进行4,643次有效运行，包括筛选、压力、保留、复制和跨提供商研究。系统操纵提示词的不同表述方式（如多重方案比较、通用思考提示、边界效率模板、误导性架构提示等），测量推理token消耗与任务正确率。

Result: 提示词表述可将推理成本乘以2.4-7.4倍（多重方案比较指令）且不改善正确性；"深入思考"提示增加1.6-2.2倍推理；边界效率模板成本中性可将推理减半。工具框架选择影响更大：相同模型-任务-提示三元组在Claude Code下每成功成本比pi高5-30倍（归因于更大的静态前缀和更多轮次）。误导性架构提示的成本远高于无关文本。在Kimi-K3和Claude Sonnet 5上的复制研究保留了主效应方向，同时揭示了模型对思考和确定性线索的特定敏感性。

Conclusion: 提示词表述和工具框架设计对编码代理成本有实质性影响，且通常不以提升任务成功率为代价。研究为成本敏感型代理部署提供了可操作的指南：避免邀请多重方案比较的提示，使用边界效率模板，谨慎选择工具框架，并注意提供商端缓存仅影响计费而非实际效率。

Abstract: Large reasoning models used as coding agents incur costs from deliberation, tool calls, and repeated agent turns, yet the causal effect of prompt wording on this spend has not been measured systematically. We present a preregistered benchmark across six large reasoning models, two real agent harnesses, and 24 deterministic coding tasks with hidden evaluators. Across 4,643 valid runs, including screening, stress, holdout, replication, and cross-provider studies, we find that prompt formulation can multiply reasoning cost without improving correctness. Asking the model to develop and compare several approaches is the most consistently wasteful instruction, increasing reasoning tokens by 2.4-7.4x across all models. Generic "think deeply" cues also increase deliberation by 1.6-2.2x, while a bounded-efficiency template specifying scope, acceptance criteria, and a stop condition is cost-neutral and can halve reasoning. Harness choice matters even more: identical model-task-prompt triples cost 5-30x more per success under Claude Code than under pi, mainly because of larger static prefixes and more turns. Misleading architectural hints are far costlier than irrelevant prose, and provider-side caching reduces billed cost without changing behavior, so it must not be treated as efficiency. Replications on Kimi-K3 and Claude Sonnet 5 preserve the main effect directions while revealing model-specific sensitivity to thinking and certainty cues. Overall, prompt wording and harness design materially affect agent cost, often with no gain in task success.

</details>


### [77] [HopRefusalBench: Diagnosing Refusal Failures in Search-Augmented Agents for Multi-Hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01358)
*Jianan Xie,Xin Sun,Zhongqi Chen,Xing Zheng,Qiang Liu,Bowen Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HopRefusalBench，这是首个针对多跳搜索场景中模型拒答（refusal）行为的受控基准测试，包含889个基于KILT实体路径构造的不可回答问题，系统评估了十个前沿模型在搜索增强模式下的拒答能力。实验发现最佳模型的靶向正确停止率（TCHR）仅为42.9%，拒绝行为在不可回答的多跳查询中仍然是一个严峻挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拒答基准测试主要暴露单跳查询表面的缺陷，无法揭示在有效的中间推理和检索之后才浮现的失效模式。多跳问题从根本上不可回答时，搜索增强的LLM代理行为尚未被充分理解，需要一个受控的基准来系统地评估这一问题。

Method: 从KILTgrounded实体路径构建889个不可回答的多跳问题，交叉三种不可回答原因（答案未知、错误前提、上下文欠指定）与三种拓扑结构（根、中间、终端节点），使前提验证、中间桥接验证和终端停止可分别观测。提出最终结果分类体系（靶向拒答、伪拒答、幻觉完成、搜索预算耗尽）和源感知轨迹指标来衡量触发后延续和令牌浪费。

Result: 在十个前沿专有和开源模型（搜索增强模式）中，最佳模型的TCHR仅为42.9%。根和中间节点比终端节点更难，所有模型在错误前提上取得最高TCHR，在上下文欠指定问题上得分最低。然而，当按类别汇总时，每个模型84.7-98.4%的明确拒答类响应识别了正确理由，说明主要瓶颈在于未能承诺适当的非回答，而非识别问题。

Conclusion: 多跳搜索中的拒答是一个重要的评估问题，为诊断和提升搜索增强代理的可靠性提供了基础。主要瓶颈在于模型难以做出适当的非回答承诺，失败轨迹倾向于分化成幻觉或搜索预算耗尽。

Abstract: Search-augmented large language model agents are increasingly capable of solving knowledge-intensive tasks, but their behavior when a multi-hop question is fundamentally unanswerable remains poorly understood. Existing abstention benchmarks largely expose defects at the surface of single-hop queries and therefore cannot reveal failures that emerge only after valid intermediate reasoning and retrieval. We introduce HopRefusalBench, the first controlled benchmark of refusal within multi-hop search, comprising 889 unanswerable questions constructed from KILT-grounded entity paths. It crosses three causes of unanswerability (answer unknown, false premise, and underspecified context) with root, middle, and terminal topologies, making premise verification, intermediate-bridge validation, and terminal stopping separately observable. We further propose a final-outcome taxonomy spanning target-aware refusal, pseudo-refusal, hallucinated completion, and search-budget exhaustion, together with source-aware trajectory metrics for post-trigger continuation and token waste. Across ten frontier proprietary and open-weight models in search-augmented mode, the best model achieves a target-aware correct halting rate (TCHR) of only 42.9%. Root and middle items are consistently harder than terminal items, and all models attain their highest TCHR on false premises and their lowest on underspecified questions. Yet when pooled across categories, 84.7--98.4% of each model's explicit refusal-like responses identify the correct rationale, localizing the main bottleneck to committing to an appropriate non-answer; failed trajectories instead diverge into hallucination or search-budget exhaustion. These results establish refusal in multi-hop search as a consequential evaluation problem and provide a foundation for diagnosing and improving the reliability of search-augmented agents.

</details>


### [78] [EviSD: Evidence-Conditioned Self-Distillation for Search-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01359)
*Jianan Xie,Xin Sun,Zhongqi Chen,Xing Zheng,Shu Wu,Bowen Song,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EviSD框架，利用实例级支持证据作为特权信息进行证据条件自蒸馏，将教师-学生差距转化为有界校正项作用于结果驱动的GRPO优势，在不改变推理时的前提下提升搜索增强语言代理的多轮问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习只能利用可验证的最终答案进行轨迹级信用分配，无法在多轮搜索过程中区分单个动作的贡献，这限制了搜索增强语言代理的细粒度学习能力。

Method: EviSD采用证据条件自蒸馏框架：将实例级支持证据作为搜索动作的特权信息，将黄金答案作为答案动作的互补特权。训练时，学生从原始上下文采样动作，同一模型作为特权教师在动作对齐的上下文下重新评分。将教师-学生差距转换为结果导出的GRPO优势的有界校正项，仅应用于生成的动作跨度上。

Result: 在七个问答基准和三个不同规模与代际的骨干模型上，EviSD在所有评估设置中取得最高的宏平均Exact Match，比最强对比方法高1.3-2.3个百分点，同时仅调节6.7%-15.1%的响应token。

Conclusion: EviSD通过局域化特权引导并保留结果奖励决定的更新方向，无需辅助蒸馏目标或在推理时做任何修改，即可显著提升搜索增强语言代理的多轮问答性能，验证了利用证据作为特权信息进行动作级信用分配的有效性。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning enables search-augmented language agents to learn from verifiable final answers, but its trajectory-level credit cannot distinguish the contributions of individual actions in a multi-turn search process. We propose EviSD, an evidence-conditioned self-distillation framework that uses instance-level supporting evidence as privileged information for search actions and golden answers as complementary privilege for answer actions. During training, the student samples actions from the original context, while the same model re-scores them as a privileged teacher under an action-aligned context. EviSD converts the detached teacher--student gap into a bounded correction to the outcome-derived GRPO advantage and applies it only to generated action spans. This design localizes privileged guidance while preserving the update direction determined by the outcome reward, without an auxiliary distillation objective or any change at inference time. Across seven question-answering benchmarks and three backbones spanning model scales and generations, EviSD achieves the highest macro-average Exact Match in all evaluated settings, outperforming the strongest compared methods by 1.3--2.3 points while modulating only 6.7%--15.1% of response tokens. Code is available at https://github.com/JiananXie/EviSD.

</details>


### [79] [Language Equality has a Price: A Systematic Investigation of Multi-turn LLM Performance for EU-24+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01395)
*Sherzod Hakimov,Karl Osswald,Jelle Psurek,Eszter Bukovszky,A. Altar Lüser,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过30种语言（含欧盟24种官方语言）的自对弈目标导向对话游戏，评估大语言模型作为语言智能体的多轮、免参考、程序化评分能力。研究发现商业系统在所有欧盟官方语言上均超越所有开源模型，且即使某些语言公共网页文本少四个数量级时商业系统仍保持领先；模型所属地区虽提升其母语表现但不足以缩小差距；此外非英语语言平均运行成本高31%、得分低10%，说明覆盖不等于服务均等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估多为静态或基于偏好的单轮任务，难以衡量模型在多语言、多轮、真实目标导向交互中的能力。作者希望提出一种语言无关的、可程序化评分的评估范式，用以公平比较不同语言间的模型表现，并揭示多语言覆盖与实际服务质量之间的差距。

Method: 设计语言无关的目标导向对话游戏作为评估框架，模型作为语言智能体参与自我对弈；通过本地化固定的提示词和词表文件扩展至新语言。在30种语言（24种欧盟官方语言加6种其他语言）上评估9个开源和商业LLM，采用多轮、免参考、程序化评分方式。

Result: 1）商业模型在欧盟24种官方语言中都全面超越所有开源模型，两个最弱的开源模型在欧盟24语平均分低于40；2）商业模型甚至在公共网页文本少四个数量级的语言中仍保持领先；3）模型所属地区仅提升其母语表现但无法缩小差距（两个中国模型的汉语为30语中最强，但全模型汉语最佳成绩来自美国商业系统）；4）非英语语言平均运行成本比英语高31%，得分低10%。

Conclusion: 商业系统展示了多语言表现均等性的可行性，但这种均等性不能仅依靠公共网络爬取数据实现；模型的多语言覆盖并不等于平等的服务质量，语言间的性能与成本差异仍然显著，需关注多语言服务的效率和公平性问题。

Abstract: We evaluate large language models (LLMs) as language agents playing goal-directed dialogue games in self-play across 30 languages: the 24 official EU languages plus six others. Unlike static or preference-based evaluation, this paradigm is multi-turn, reference-free and programmatically scored, and because the game mechanics are language-agnostic it extends to a new language by localising a fixed set of prompt and word-list files. Evaluating nine open-weight and commercial LLMs, we find that no open-weight model covers the EU-24 well: in every official language both commercial systems outscore every open-weight model, and the two weakest average below 40 points across the EU-24. The commercial systems stay ahead even in languages with four orders of magnitude less public web text, showing that linguistic parity is achievable, but not from public crawls alone. A model's home region lifts it without closing the gap: Chinese is the strongest of all 30 languages for two Chinese-developed models, yet the best Chinese score of any model belongs to a US commercial system. Coverage is also not parity of service. Pooled over models and languages, the median non-English language costs 31% more to run than English, and scores 10% lower.

</details>


### [80] [When Retrieval Helps and Distracts: Evaluating Evidence-Generating LLMs for Biomedical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01409)
*Pritam Deka,Prabhjot Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生物医学事实验证系统中的证据生成问题，提出了统一基准CARE-XAI，对比了多种模型架构，并引入了新的诊断指标Bio-GRACE来评估检索证据对决策的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学事实验证系统不仅需要预测主张是否被支持、反驳或未涉及，还应生成忠实、完整且对验证有用的证据。现有的评估框架缺乏对这一证据生成环节的系统性研究。

Method: 在CARE-XAI统一基准（涵盖五个生物医学与健康事实验证来源）上，采用共享评估协议，对比基础指令LLM、PubMed检索增强LLM、微调LLM、仅标签LLM以及生物医学编码器分类器。并引入Bio-GRACE（gold-reference-normalized诊断指标）衡量检索证据是否能恢复参考证据的决策收益。

Result: 生物医学分类器在仅判断标签方面最强；微调LLM在证据生成上最强。PubMed检索效果具有来源依赖性：对PubMedQA和SciFact等PubMed对齐的来源有帮助，但会分散模型对更广泛的公共卫生主张的注意力。Bio-GRACE显示检索效用依赖来源、支持选择性检索策略，并揭示检索召回率和词汇级证据重叠不足以支撑生物医学事实验证。

Conclusion: 证据生成是生物医学事实验证的重要维度，不同模型在此任务上表现各异；检索增强并非普遍有益，需要来源自适应和选择性的检索策略；Bio-GRACE为衡量检索证据效用提供了更有效的诊断工具。

Abstract: Biomedical fact-checking systems must do more than predict whether a claim is supported, contradicted, or unaddressed: they should also produce evidence that is faithful, complete, and useful for verification. We study this evidence-generation setting on CARE-XAI, a unified benchmark spanning five biomedical and health fact-checking sources. We compare base instruction LLMs, PubMed retrieval-augmented LLMs, fine-tuned LLMs, label-only LLMs, and biomedical encoder classifiers under a shared evaluation protocol. Biomedical classifiers remain strongest for verdict-only prediction, while fine-tuned LLMs are the strongest evidence-generating systems. PubMed retrieval is mixed: it helps PubMed-aligned sources such as PubMedQA and SciFact, but can distract models on broader public-health claims. We introduce Bio-GRACE, a gold-reference-normalized diagnostic for measuring whether retrieved evidence recovers the decision benefit of reference evidence. Bio-GRACE shows that retrieval utility is source-dependent, motivates selective retrieval, and exposes why retrieval recall and lexical evidence overlap are insufficient for biomedical fact-checking.

</details>


### [81] [QR-Erase: Efficient Subspace-Based Machine Unlearning with Layer Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01422)
*Tyler Lizzo,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QR-Erase，一种基于Pivoted QR分解的子空间机器遗忘框架，无需SVD即可高效识别和移除模型参数中的任务特定表示，并进一步通过Layer-Localized QR-Erase限制在任务信息最集中层中的更新，在多种遗忘任务中实现了优于优化方法且接近SVD性能的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法存在两大瓶颈：基于优化的方法会损害模型的无关能力，而基于子空间的方法（如SVD）计算开销过大。因此需要一种既能精确恢复任务相关子空间、又具有计算高效性的替代方案。

Method: 提出Pivoted QR分解替代SVD来识别和移除模型参数中的任务特定表示，并引入Layer-Localized QR-Erase变体，仅对包含最高浓度任务信息的层进行参数更新，利用低秩和层局部化结构来提升遗忘效果。

Result: 理论证明Pivoted QR提供有界误差的精确子空间恢复，且在温和谱隙条件下逼近最优SVD解。在任务级、跨语言和语音遗忘实验中，QR-Erase在遗忘-保留权衡上优于优化方法，所有指标与SVD差距在5%以内；利用低秩和层局部化结构可进一步提升遗忘效果（如语音遗忘集准确率从53.1%降到15.7%）。

Conclusion: 精确的子空间恢复而非最优重构即可实现有效的机器遗忘，QR-Erase为现代基础模型提供了一种高效、通用的SVD替代方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove targeted information from trained models without requiring costly retraining. Existing optimization-based methods often degrade unrelated capabilities, while subspace-based approaches rely on computationally expensive singular value decompositions (SVD). We introduce QR-Erase, a subspace-based framework that uses Pivoted QR decomposition to identify and remove task-specific representations directly from model parameters. We further propose Layer-Localized QR-Erase, which restricts updates to layers containing the highest concentration of task-specific information. We show that Pivoted QR provides accurate subspace recovery with bounded error, and that under a mild spectral gap condition, the recovered subspace approaches the optimal SVD solution. Across task-level, cross-lingual, and speech unlearning, QR-Erase achieves a stronger forgetting-retention tradeoff than optimization-based methods while remaining within 5% of SVD across all metrics. Exploiting low-rank and layer-localized structure further improves forgetting (for example, reducing speech forget-set accuracy from 53.1% to 15.7%). These results demonstrate that accurate subspace recovery, rather than optimal reconstruction, is sufficient for effective unlearning and provides an efficient and general alternative to SVD-based methods for modern foundation models.

</details>


### [82] [PALMs: Using Multi Construct-Grounded Rationales for Modeling Population Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01458)
*Priyanka Dey,Brihi Joshi,Preyashi Poddar,Jieyu Zhao,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出种群对齐语言模型（PALMs），通过在偏好调优过程中引入基于心理和文化构念的合成rationales作为潜在监督，将LLM对齐到特定国家人群（美国、印度、巴西、法国、意大利）。在人格、价值观、文化规范和道德四个维度上，PALMs相比基线模型平均提升8.59%，并在个性化奖励建模、种群模拟和社交推理等下游任务上展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被广泛用于模拟个体用户行为，但忠实表征一个群体需要捕捉不同群体之间在价值观、信念和文化规范上的系统性差异。现有方法缺乏对心理和文化维度的结构化建模，导致模型难以精准对齐特定人群。

Method: PALMs通过两个核心步骤构建：首先，合成根植于心理学和文化构念的rationales（推理依据）；然后，将这些rationales作为潜监督信号，在偏好调优（preference tuning）过程中实现针对特定人群的对齐。

Result: 在人格、价值观与信念、文化规范、道德四个评估维度上，PALMs一致优于包括文化专门化模型在内的所有基线，五个种群上平均相对提升8.59%。基于构念的rationales优于人口统计提示和基于调查数据的微调方法。在下游任务中，个性化奖励建模相对提升5.19%，种群模拟提升6.34%，并可迁移至社交推理任务。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将偏好学习扎根于心理学和文化构念，而非仅依赖表层的响应分布，能提供更丰富的归纳信号，从而更有效地实现LLM的人群级对齐，且无需特定任务的监督即可泛化到多种下游应用。

Abstract: Large language models are being extensively used to simulate individual user behavior, yet faithfully representing a population requires capturing the systematic variation in values, beliefs, and cultural norms that distinguish one group from another. We introduce Population Aligned Language Models (PALMs), a suite of models each aligned to specific populations, covering five countries: USA, India, Brazil, France and Italy. PALMs are created by synthesizing rationales grounded in psychological and cultural constructs and using these as latent supervision during preference tuning for population-specific alignment. Evaluated across four dimensions: personality, values and beliefs, cultural norms, and morality, PALMs consistently outperform baselines, including culture-specialized models, achieving an average of 8.59% relative improvement over the best baseline across all five populations. Notably, construct-grounded rationales outperform both demographic prompting and survey-based fine-tuning, suggesting that grounding preference learning in psychology and culture provides a richer inductive signal than surface-level response distributions. We further demonstrate strong generalization to downstream applications with- out task-specific supervision: outperforming best baselines by 5.19% in personalized reward modeling, 6.34% in population simulation, and showing strong transfer to social reasoning tasks. Datasets and code are available at: https://github.com/limenlp/PALMs.

</details>


### [83] [Retrieval Augmented Biomedical Question Answering with Weak Question Recovery and Neural Reranking for BioASQ Task 14b](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01468)
*Xueying Zhao,Lee Mai,Balaji Anandganesh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT ARC BioASQ团队构建的生物医学问答系统，该系统整合了多源查询扩展、神经重排序、检索精炼和OpenBioLLM答案生成。系统采用PubMed检索结合微调MiniLM语义重排序、Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF)和基于特征的相关性评分来提升文档排名质量。针对弱检索性能的困难查询，提出条件性弱查询恢复策略，包括语义扩展、关系感知增强和选择性结果合并。检索后修剪阶段进一步移除冗余或低相关性片段，同时保留证据覆盖用于下游答案生成。在BioASQ评估批次上的实验表明所提策略显著提升了困难问题集的检索鲁棒性和MAP@10性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学问答中的检索系统在困难查询上表现不佳，需要提升文档排序质量和检索鲁棒性，以确保最终答案生成的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出一个多阶段流水线：(1) PubMed检索与多源查询扩展；(2) 基于微调MiniLM的神经语义重排序；结合RRF和特征相关性评分；(3) 条件性弱查询恢复策略——针对检索不佳的查询应用语义扩展、关系感知增强和选择性结果合并；(4) 检索后修剪阶段移除冗余片段；(5) OpenBioLLM辅助答案生成，并配以输出验证和后处理以保证格式一致性。

Result: 在BioASQ评估批次上的实验表明，所提出的弱查询恢复和检索后清理策略在困难问题集上显著提升了检索鲁棒性和MAP@10性能。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了端到端的生物医学问答流水线，通过多策略检索增强和精炼有效应对困难查询，并在BioASQ各阶段中保持提交可靠性和格式一致性。

Abstract: This work presents DS@GT ARC BioASQ team's work for a biomedical question answering pipeline, integrating multi-source query expansion, neural reranking, retrieval refinement, and OpenBioLLM-assisted answer generation. The system combines PubMed retrieval with fine-tuned MiniLM-based semantic reranking, Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), and feature-based relevance scoring to improve document ranking quality. To address challenging queries with weak retrieval performance, we introduce a conditional weak-question recovery strategy that applies semantic expansion, relationship-aware augmentation, and selective result merging. A post-retrieval pruning stage further removes redundant or low-relevance snippets while preserving evidence coverage for downstream answer generation. Experimental results on BioASQ evaluation batches demonstrate that the proposed recovery and cleanup strategies substantially improve retrieval robustness and MAP@10 performance on difficult question sets. The final system also incorporates output validation and post-processing steps to ensure formatting consistency and submission reliability across BioASQ phases.

</details>


### [84] [Two-Stage Bengali Sentiment Classification: Domain Adaptation Through Continual Learning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01471)
*MD Shaikh Rahman,Syed Maudud E Rabbi,Muhammad Mahbubur Rashid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SentiBanglaBERT，一个用于孟加拉语情感分类的两阶段框架，结合领域自适应持续预训练与LoRA参数高效微调，并集成SHAP可解释性分析，在低资源语言场景下实现了媲美强基线的性能，同时提供了更深的语言可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（尤其是孟加拉语）的情感分析面临领域数据稀缺的挑战，现有方法在性能与可解释性之间存在权衡。作者旨在构建一个既高效又透明的情感分类框架，以满足形态丰富但资源不足语言的NLP需求。

Method: 采用两阶段架构：第一阶段进行领域自适应持续预训练（domain-adaptive continual pretraining），使模型适应新闻类数据；第二阶段通过Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)进行参数高效微调，降低计算开销。同时集成SHAP（Shapley Additive Explanations）进行可解释性分析，揭示孟加拉语形态学线索（如否定后缀和体标记）对情感预测的影响。

Result: 实验表明SentiBanglaBERT在性能上与强基线相当且表现稳定，同时提供了更优的透明度和解释深度。

Conclusion: 领域自适应持续学习是构建可解释、资源高效的NLP系统的有效基础，尤其适用于形态丰富且资源不足的语言（如孟加拉语）。该框架展示了在低资源场景下兼顾性能与可解释性的可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding sentiment in low-resource languages remains a key challenge for Natural Language Processing (NLP), particularly when domain-specific data is scarce. In this work, we present SentiBanglaBERT, a two-stage Bengali sentiment classification framework combining domain-adaptive continual pretraining and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The approach enables contextual adaptation to news-style data while remaining computationally efficient through Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Beyond performance, SentiBanglaBERT integrates SHAP-based interpretability, offering linguistic insights into how Bengali morphological cues, such as negation suffixes and aspectual markers, influence sentiment predictions. Experiments demonstrate stable performance comparable to strong baselines while providing greater transparency and interpretive depth. This framework highlights the potential of domain-adaptive continual learning as a foundation for interpretable, resource-efficient NLP in morphologically rich, underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [85] [Discriminative Axis, Not Data Volume: What a Contrastive Corpus Teaches an Audio Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01560)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现对比学习中扩大语料规模无法帮助编码缺失的属性，真正起决定作用的是语料的结构（批次内负样本的可分离轴）。通过在冻结基础的多模态嵌入模型中加入词法-语音轮次，零样本关键词识别提升76点但语音情感识别下降14点；使用受控语料微调可恢复情感性能，而挖掘的情感标注语料即使规模更大也无效。干预实验证实，降低标题多样性使情感成为唯一可分轴可恢复8.9点性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为扩大语料规模是缓解对比表示缺失属性的默认手段，但研究者发现存在语料扩增无效的情况，需要识别真正决定对比学习能否编码某属性的因素，以指导有效的数据工程。

Method: 采用三组对比实验：1) 在冻结基础多模态嵌入模型中添加词法-语音轮次并评估零样本关键词识别与语音情感识别；2) 使用7,442条韵律受控语料微调以检验容量限制假设，以及29,428条挖掘的情感命名标题语料以检验数据量假设；3) 对同一音频进行干预——提高标题相似度和折叠标题多样性，验证因果性。

Result: 加入词法-语音轮次使关键词识别提升76点但情感识别下降14点；受控语料微调恢复情感性能仅损失5点关键词精度；挖掘语料即使曝光量匹配也对情感性能几乎无影响（-0.0007）；折叠标题多样性使情感识别回升8.9点（三种随机种子平均），关键词精度相应回退。

Conclusion: 决定对比音频嵌入编码何种属性的核心因素是语料结构（批次内负样本能否在不使用目标属性的情况下被分离），而非语料规模或标题词汇量。受控语料通过固定句子内容迫使韵律成为唯一分离信号，从而成功编码情感属性。

Abstract: Scaling the corpus is the default remedy when a contrastive representation lacks an attribute. We report a case where it does nothing, and identify what does: adding a lexical-speech round to a frozen-base multimodal embedding model raises zero-shot keyword spotting by 76 points while reducing speech-emotion recognition by 14. The loss is not a capacity limit: fine-tuning on 7,442 clips from a prosody-controlled corpus recovers emotion past its pre-speech level at a five-point keyword cost. Nor is it data volume: 29,428 mined clips whose captions explicitly name emotions, at matched exposure, move emotion by -0.0007. The difference is structural: a contrastive objective encodes an attribute only when the in-batch negatives cannot be separated without it; the controlled corpus holds sentence content fixed, so prosody is the only separating signal, whereas mined captions name emotion yet remain separable by scene content. Intervention on the same audio confirms causality: raising caption similarity does not recover emotion, but collapsing caption diversity so that emotion becomes the only separating axis recovers it by 8.9 points across three seeds, with a smaller, same-signed gain on a non-acted corpus, while keyword accuracy trades back. Corpus structure, not size or caption vocabulary, controls what a contrastive audio embedding encodes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过"守恒一致编码"(CCE)框架重新审视莱布尼茨的磨坊、图灵测试和塞尔的中文房间论证，将行为表现(W_causal,T)与操作性意识(κ_T)分离，指出压缩生成系统与未压缩查找系统在行为上可比但在内部组织效率上截然不同，为AI安全分析提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的心智哲学思想实验（莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、塞尔中文房间）长期争论"理解"的本质，但缺乏形式化工具将行为表现与内部结构组织区分开来。作者希望用CCE框架厘清这一混淆，从而为AI安全问题提供更明确的哲学基础。

Method: 作者在玩具符号设定中形式化了两个度量：任务表现W_causal,T衡量行为成功，操作性意识κ_T衡量保留内部结构支撑该行为的效率。通过对比一个未压缩查找系统（依赖不断扩大的未复用映射库）与一个紧凑生成系统（复用紧凑内部结构）来展示二者的分化。

Result: 未压缩查找系统和紧凑生成系统在行为成功上可以达到可比水平，但在κ_T指标上差异显著：前者依赖不断膨胀的未复用映射存储，后者重用紧凑的内部结构。这证明行为表现与支撑它的内部组织可以相互分离。

Conclusion: 该框架将关于"理解"的经典争论重构为对外在表现与维持该表现的内部组织之间的区分，并指出这一区分对后续AI安全分析具有潜在意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [87] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoFOAM，一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的自进化智能体，能够根据自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和演化OpenFOAM仿真流程，通过7阶段的进化循环和三重抗退化机制，大幅降低CFD仿真的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）虽在现代工程中作用重要，但使用门槛高，需要大量专业知识和技能，且配置文件设置耗时费力。作者旨在通过生成式AI降低这一负担，让非专家也能进行高级CFD仿真。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B预训练模型，在252个文本提示（覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y-plus感知数值策略）上进行微调。核心是7阶段的进化循环算法，并引入三种互补的抗退化流：RAG增强的检索重试上下文、外科手术式字典级修补、提示多样性释义。

Result: AutoFOAM能够仅凭自然语言指令自主完成OpenFOAM仿真全流程，通过进化循环持续自我改进，并借助三种抗退化机制防止重复自训练导致的模型退化，实现了生成式AI与严谨流体仿真的有效桥接。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM加速了CFD快速原型设计流程，并使高级CFD工作流民主化，为非专业用户提供了便捷的仿真入口，展示了LLM智能体在科学计算工程领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [88] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在中小企业（SME）环境中使用检索增强生成（RAG）技术来缓解大语言模型（LLM）幻觉问题的方法，提出了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种建模方案，并在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多个主流LLM上进行实验评估。结果表明RAG增强的LLM能显著提升响应质量，减少幻觉和错误信息，支持更可靠、可信和上下文感知的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在中小企业中被广泛用于问答和决策支持，但幻觉问题会产生错误信息，降低用户对系统可靠性及可信度的信心。需要一种有效方法来缓解这一风险。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种RAG建模方法，通过引入外部知识源来增强LLM的生成过程，并在多个主流LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上评估有效性。

Result: 实验证明RAG增强的LLM显著提升了有用响应生成能力，降低了幻觉和错误信息风险，提升了上下文相关性和人类可解释性。

Conclusion: RAG增强技术（包括向量检索和图检索两种实现）能够有效缓解LLM幻觉问题，在SME环境中实现更可靠、可信赖且上下文感知的决策支持。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [89] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的、硬件级别的LLM能耗基准测试方法，在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上对9个开源LLM（1B~7B参数）进行了能耗评估，使用Ollama推理引擎和nvidia-smi以2Hz采样GPU功耗。结果显示gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低（0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token），吞吐量最高（>170 tok/s），而7B-Mistral模型每token能耗比最高效模型高出4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因内部推理导致单prompt能耗异常偏高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着隐私关注和对本地推理的需求，LLM本地部署日益普及，但消费级硬件上的能源成本尚未得到充分表征。现有基准大多只关注模型准确性，忽略了能耗这一重要维度，因此亟需一个可复现的硬件级能耗基准来填补该空白。

Method: 在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上，通过Ollama推理引擎执行9个开源LLM（1B至7B参数），使用nvidia-smi以2Hz频率对GPU功耗进行采样，并在固定提示集上评估平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出token能耗（J/token）以及吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: 研究发现除原始参数数量外，模型架构和量化策略也驱动能源效率。gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b实现最低能耗（0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）和最高吞吐量（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral每token能耗是最优模型的4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展的内部推理导致每prompt能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 该基准强调效率指标需区分token生成模式，单纯以参数数量预测能耗不够准确，架构选择和量化策略对能源效率影响显著，为消费级硬件上的LLM部署提供了能耗优化的参考依据。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [90] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core，一种无需训练的核问题选择框架，通过零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹将问题投影到潜在空间，按内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度进行聚类，从而在降低LLM评估计算开销的同时保持高保真度评分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在持续开发过程中的评估计算开销巨大。现有核心集选择方法存在两个关键瓶颈：一是需要大量历史日志的"冷启动"问题（如项目反应理论），二是存在表面词汇偏差，无法捕捉任务的深层推理流形。

Method: CoT-Core框架：1) 利用LLM展开零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹；2) 将这些推理路径投影到潜在空间；3) 基于内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度对问题聚类，实现无需训练和无需历史日志的核心问题选择。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA四个基准上的实验表明，CoT-Core能大幅降低评估成本同时保持高保真度评分估计；此外还揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，即其有效性受任务复杂度的内在门控。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过利用CoT推理轨迹实现了低成本的LLM高保真评估，避免了冷启动问题并克服了表面词汇偏差；但推理感知剪枝的效果并非普遍适用，而是与任务复杂度密切相关。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [91] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线，结合自主合成的500个优化问题数据集（基于Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述和LLM生成的专家角色构建），以提升LLM在优化建模任务中的表现。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下，该流水线在NL4OPT上准确率从40%提升至72%，在MAMO Easy上从40%提升至56%，在MAMO Complex上从32%提升至56%，表明语义验证的合成示例结合检索增强是微调的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专业知识和建模语言熟练度，而现有大语言模型在生成优化公式时经常产生结构不一致或不完整的输出，尤其在组合优化场景中问题突出。现有方法依赖昂贵的微调，因此需要探索一种无需重新训练模型的实用替代方案来提升LLM的优化建模能力。

Method: 首先利用Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述和LLM创建的专家角色（persona），合成500个优化问题，以JSON格式表示并关联已验证的Python求解器脚本。随后将这些问题编码到Chroma向量数据库中构建检索索引。推理时，对每个新问题检索语义相似的问题作为上下文指导，使用LangChain LLM智能体在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下进行建模。通过三个基准测试集（NL4OPT、MAMO Easy、MAMO Complex）评估流水线性能。

Result: 在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下，准确率从40%提升至72%（NL4OPT）、从40%提升至56%（MAMO Easy）、从32%提升至56%（MAMO Complex）。语义验证的合成示例显著提高了求解准确率和模型结构的完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强的结合是微调的有效替代方案，表明领域特定的合成语料库搭配检索增强可以作为在真实决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具的实际路径，无需昂贵的模型重训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [92] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于外部记忆的LLM智能体在无权重更新场景下的自我改进机制，揭示了"Echo Gap"失败模式：LLM自评奖励被高估的错误记忆被反复重用导致错误累积。文章形式化了"误差独立性假设"(EIA)作为纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，并提出了免答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本转SQL基准上实现了端到端一致的性能提升（56.9%执行精度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进的LLM智能体通过外部记忆存贮经验而非更新权重来学习，但部署环境中缺乏真实标签，存储的分数只能来自LLM自身的评估。这种替代会导致错误记忆获得虚高奖励，智能体反复重用自身最有信心的错误，错误通过记忆累积而非平均化消除，亟需形式化理解并解决这一核心缺陷。

Method: （1）将存储的分数视为非参数隐式策略的代理奖励，检索到的每条经验被视为一次策略改进步骤。（2）形式化"误差独立性假设"(EIA)并证明其是纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，恢复的收益是追踪真理程度与误差去相关程度两个量的闭式函数。（3）证明奖励膨胀不仅在按存储分数排序检索时发生，在纯相似性检索（部署场景）下同样存在。（4）提出免答案的去膨胀算法LUCID来修正偏差。

Result: 在BIRD文本转SQL基准上，LUCID将执行精度提升至56.9%，优于Memento式自评智能体（54.0%，跨种子平均提升+2.9个点）和无记忆的相同架构智能体（52.4%），实现了一致的端到端增益。

Conclusion: 研究表明自我评分式记忆奖励存在结构性偏差，纠正该偏差的必要条件是误差独立性——即验证信号不仅要追踪真实质量，还要与记忆偏差去相关。LUCID算法在无真实标签条件下能有效去膨胀，为记忆型自我改进智能体提供了理论保障和实用解法。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [93] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，通过离线重放请求子集并在vLLM下集成GPU功耗遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量作为基准真值，再用轻量级校准模型JCalib在服务时预测Shapley份额，实现批量LLM推理服务中请求级能耗的公平归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM服务提升吞吐量但使能耗核算复杂化：GPU功耗遥测是聚合的，而可持续性报告、计费和负载分析需要请求级能耗。现有推理能耗基准只报告模型、阶段或token级能耗，缺乏实测的请求级真值，导致实践中的能耗分配规则与公平分配的偏差程度未知。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：(1) 离线测试台通过可复现协议在vLLM下重放请求子集，集成GPU功耗遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能耗；(2) 轻量级校准模型JCalib从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，用于服务时推理。此外还使用采样Shapley将测量参考扩展到更大的请求组。

Result: 在16组模型/工作负载运行中，token比例归属与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1偏差在静态批处理下为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且在三个数据中心GPU上可复现。JCalib将误差降至静态批处理0.116和连续批处理0.177，优于在线不可用的独立测量基线，同时保持批量能耗效率。

Conclusion: token归属在批量执行下不能作为边际能耗的可靠代理；实测的Shapley基准真值可以校准低成本请求特征以实现更公平的能耗归属。采样Shapley将参考扩展到更大组规模，偏差持续存在，单一离线校准仍是最准确的可用部署规则。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [94] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba 是一种基于主题约束的低秩循环通路增强的状态空间模型，通过在三节点网络主题启发下增强状态维度间的显式交互，在保持 Mamba 线性时间递归结构的同时，在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务上均优于标准 Mamba。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力在长序列建模中呈二次方复杂度，Mamba 虽通过选择性状态空间递归实现线性时间，但其对角化状态转移限制了状态维度间的显式交互，难以充分捕捉跨维度动态信息。

Method: 受三节点网络主题动力学启发，Motif-Mamba 在 Mamba 中引入一条基于主题约束的低秩循环通路：先将隐状态投影到紧凑动力学子空间，施加主题引导的交互，再映射回原始状态空间，从而增强跨维度通信且不改变线性时间递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准和脑机接口解码实验中，Motif-Mamba 一致优于标准 Mamba 骨干模型。

Conclusion: 主题引导的低秩动力学为长程序列建模提供了一种有效的结构先验，兼顾了 Mamba 的计算效率与更强的状态间交互表达能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [95] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个自动化端到端JIT编译器，通过捕获eager执行、统一前后向传播为值语义方言、基于算术强度确定性地推导最优执行调度（无需搜索）以及基于结构哈希运行时合成细粒度kernel，实现对硬件映射的绝对控制，在RTX 3060上性能匹配或超越cuBLAS和XLA，模型级吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%，比XLA高4.4%，内存占用减少29%，可在12GB消费级GPU上训练144M参数模型（PyTorch在此场景OOM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层张量框架的eager执行模型天然缺乏全图可见性以及对硬件和内存的细粒度控制，限制了大规模深度学习模型对物理硬件的利用率，因此需要一种自动化编译器来弥合高层数学操作与底层物理硬件之间的映射鸿沟。

Method: Nova包含三个核心组件：(1) 捕获eager执行并将前向与反向传播统一为单一值语义方言，解锁全图级优化；(2) Analytic Configurator基于算术强度确定性推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降为零；(3) 基于结构哈希的运行时直接从计算结构合成细粒度kernel。整体实现fused跨操作边界融合、复杂内存层次优化及寄存器级调优。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，TF32 matmul在大多数形状下匹配或小幅超越cuBLAS和XLA（相对误差<5e-4）；模型级上，42M参数模型吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%、比XLA高4.4%；内存占用比PyTorch降低最多29%，使144M参数模型能在12GB消费级GPU上以17,900 tokens/s训练，而PyTorch在此场景发生OOM。

Conclusion: Nova证明了通过自动化JIT编译管线结合确定性分析而非启发式搜索，可以在不牺牲数值精度的前提下同时实现性能提升和内存效率的显著改善，尤其对消费级GPU上的大规模模型训练具有实际价值。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [96] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个统一的工具包与交互式Web界面，用于检测检索增强、智能体及记忆增强型LLM系统中的上下文幻觉，统一了三种检测器范式（表征探测、不确定性估计、裁判式验证）与生成前查询可回答性任务，支持白盒/黑盒场景下的响应级和跨度级检查，并提供可视化分析界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强、智能体及记忆增强型LLM系统容易出现上下文幻觉——即输出流畅合理但缺乏证据支持的内容。现有工具缺乏统一的框架来整合多种检测方法和任务（如查询可回答性评估），难以进行系统化的幻觉检测与比较。

Method: SIRIN统一了三种检测器范式（表征探测、不确定性估计、裁判式验证），并结合生成前查询可回答性判断。它提供统一的接口、配置系统和评估流水线，支持响应级与跨度级检查，兼容白盒和黑盒设置。Web UI支持用户提供的上下文-查询-答案三元组的实时分析、幻觉分数显示、未支持跨度高亮及检测器并排比较，并采用轻量级插件化设计便于扩展新检测器。

Result: SIRIN在幻觉检测、查询可回答性和长期记忆系统中的忠实度门控等任务上进行了演示，源码已公开于GitHub。

Conclusion: SIRIN提供了一个统一的、可扩展的幻觉检测与检查平台，集成了多种检测范式和可回答性评估，并通过交互式Web界面增强了可解释性和可用性，有助于提升RAG、智能体及记忆增强LLM系统的可靠性与忠实性。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [97] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比表征向量提取和因果干预实验，证明视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的视觉表征并非静态信息库，而是会被语言提示动态重塑——具体表现为"抽象参考表征"（标记目标相关对象）和"属性调制"（放大目标相关属性）两种机制，且这些调制因果性地影响模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM广泛用于目标分类、搜索等目标导向视觉任务，但其视觉表征是否会被目标导向语言动态重编码这一问题尚不清楚。先前研究大多将VLM中的视觉表征视为被语言表征操作的静态信息仓库，缺乏对跨模态动态交互的深入理解。

Method: 1）提取与自然语言提示下目标相关对象对应的抽象参考表征，构建对比steering vectors，验证其对模型预测的因果作用；2）通过分析深层网络层，观察语言提示对视觉表征中目标相关属性的选择性放大；3）进行因果干预实验，证明属性调制中介了VLM的响应分布。

Result: 发现两种语言诱导的视觉表征重编码机制：抽象参考表征可泛化至不同对象、不同任务情境，甚至从合成图像泛化到自然图像；属性调制在深层网络层中发生且跨多种提示一致存在。因果干预进一步证实这些调制效应确实导致了模型输出的改变。

Conclusion: VLM中的视觉token并非静态信息储存器，而是会根据语言查询动态调制以支持下游任务。这一发现支持跨模态处理更动态的观点，为理解VLM的内部表征机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [98] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RagTester，一种面向RAG系统的自动化端到端测试方法。该方法自动生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出，使用LLM作为评判者评估系统回答。在24种配置、72,000次测试执行中，RagTester检测到21,633个失败，比基准方法多6.6%，并在24种配置中的20种中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的可靠性取决于生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构造策略之间的复杂交互，而现有方法缺乏对这种交互进行系统性端到端测试的手段，导致RAG系统在部署前难以全面评估。

Method: RagTester采用自动化的端到端测试流程：(1) 生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出；(2) 执行测试；(3) 使用LLM作为judge评估回答质量。其测试生成策略针对复杂段落、不支持的查询和文档覆盖率三个维度设计。

Result: 使用8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成24种兼容配置进行评测，通过与基准测试输入生成器对比，在72,000次测试执行中检测到21,633个失败，比基准多6.6%，并在20/24种配置中优于基准。检测到的失败类型包括：不准确的检索、不支持的答案、未充分利用检索上下文、难以解释复杂段落。

Conclusion: 证明面向覆盖率的测试生成方法能有效暴露由检索与生成组件交互导致的失败，支持RAG系统在部署前对其配置进行评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [99] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出H+ Embedding，一种统一的多粒度检索器，通过预测可变长度的短语分区、保留未覆盖token为单例，并采用重要性引导的单元选择和加权MaxSim交互，在全局向量与token级交互之间找到了中间质量-成本平衡点。在16个科学、医学和双语任务上，短语检索分支相比全局检索分支提升6.91 macro nDCG@10，同时比Token方法节省13.7%的文档向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索表示处于两个极端：单向量检索器过度压缩局部相关信号，无法保留多词实体、缩写、数值约束和组合概念等术语密集型信息；而token级延迟交互虽保留细节但引入大量索引、存储和评分成本。这两者之间的不对称引出一个核心问题：上下文相关的短语能否作为介于全局向量和token之间的有效检索单元。

Method: H+ Embedding是一个统一的多粒度检索器，包含三个关键组件：(1) 预测可变长度短语分区，将输入划分为语义完整的短语单元；(2) 将未被划分覆盖的token保留为单例单元，确保信息不丢失；(3) 采用重要性引导的单元选择与加权MaxSim交互机制，在控制计算成本的同时优化相关性评分。

Result: 在16个科学、医学和双语任务上，短语检索分支比全局检索分支在macro nDCG@10上高出6.91；相比Token方法，仅使用13.7%更少的文档向量就能接近其性能；在中等级向量预算下优于基于内容无关的分组规则。

Conclusion: 上下文相关的短语交互为实际检索系统提供了一个介于全局压缩和token级交互之间的中间质量-成本平衡点，特别适用于术语密集型场景（如医学检索）中对多词实体、缩写和数值约束的保真需求。

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [100] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于2026年6月GitHub Copilot的生产环境轨迹数据（320万用户、1300万会话、7.61亿次LLM调用、95万亿token），首次对AI编码智能体（如GitHub Copilot、Claude Code、Codex）的工作负载进行了生产规模的特征分析，揭示了与聊天机器人截然不同的工作负载特性，并设计了轻量级空闲时间预测器用于资源编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体将多步LLM推理与工具执行交错进行，形成了与聊天机器人不同的工作负载，但现有LLM服务系统大多基于聊天场景设计，缺乏对智能体工作负载的理解和针对性优化，因此需要生产规模的特征刻画。

Method: 使用2026年6月采样得到的GitHub Copilot生产轨迹数据（包含3.2M用户、13M会话、761M次LLM调用和95T tokens），对智能体编码会话的结构、KV缓存命中率、token消耗、会话时长、工具调用等维度进行系统的统计分析，并设计了一个轻量级的空闲时间预测器。

Result: 研究发现：智能体编码会话由稀疏的用户发起轮次组成，每轮展开为自主智能体循环，LLM调用几乎总伴随工具执行；轮次内KV缓存命中率平均达90%，但跨轮次边界降至55%，在模型切换或上下文压缩等事件后大幅失效；工作流和用户行为呈现多样性和长尾分布特征；智能体的快速周转时间与轮次边界处数分钟的用户空闲期形成鲜明对比，所设计的预测器可捕获86-90%的总空闲时间。

Conclusion: 这些发现挑战了当前LLM服务系统的假设基础，为构建面向智能体的（agent-native）基础设施提供了实证依据。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [101] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bazaar——一个动态密封投标基准，用于在隐藏客户偏好、实时竞争和需求冲击条件下评估LLM代理的多属性定价能力。测试11个前沿LLM后发现：客户获取领先者（Gemini 3.1 Pro）与利润领先者（Opus 4.6）不一致，需求冲击后学习最快的代理反而最慢更新信念，且最强代理仅捕获不到三分之一的事后最优利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体商务正从概念走向真实部署，支付网络、零售商和AI平台正为代理代行交易铺路。然而，LLM代理能否在真实市场（客户端偏好隐藏、对手实时适应、需求可能突变）中胜任定价，尚未被系统性测试。

Method: 构建Bazaar，一个动态密封投标的多属性拍卖基准。该基准虽具动态性，但基于闭式客户效用函数，可实现精确评估。对来自四个提供商的11个前沿LLM进行系统性测试。

Result: （1）客户获取领先的代理（Gemini 3.1 Pro）并非利润领先者（Opus 4.6），排名不一致；（2）需求冲击下排名再次改变，冲击前学习最快的代理在冲击后修订信念最慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro恢复最快但利润不领先；（3）即使最强代理也只捕获不到三分之一的事后最优利润。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在智能体商务方向有所进展，但定价能力仍存在显著提升空间，与事后最优利润之间存在巨大鸿沟。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions.
