We propose Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding (LF3DRoPE), which expresses inter-residue displacements in backbone-defined local frames and injects them directly into rotary attention. This design preserves continuous directional geometry while ensuring invariance to global $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations. On the AsEP benchmark, LF3DRoPE achieves state-of-the-art $\mathrm{MCC}$ on both ratio and epitope-group splits. Ablations and rigid transformation tests show that local three-dimensional geometry provides information beyond sequence-order attention while preserving invariance to arbitrary global coordinate systems. Mutation ranking results further indicate that LF3DRoPE captures antigen-specific structural compatibility.

</details>


### [166] [Fighting Fire with Fire: On the Feasibility of Protecting Exercises Against AI Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01112)
*Tobias Braun,Jonas Grebe,Louis Rethfeld,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用对抗机器学习技术保护教育评估的完整性：通过对多模态选择题的视觉组件施加微妙的对抗扰动，诱导AI求解器产生特定错误答案，形成统计指纹，从而检测学生对AI的盲目依赖。研究在现实黑箱假设下测试了Claude、Gemini和ChatGPT三种主流多模态模型，通过替代模型优化对抗扰动，并利用统计假设检验实现原理性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的普及使学生能够将认知努力外包给越来越强大的助手，造成一种能力幻觉，同时削弱了教育旨在培养的独立推理能力。现有教育评估方法无法有效区分真实理解与AI辅助下的表面答案，亟需一种能够保护教育练习免受这种侵蚀性依赖的机制。

Method: 提出对抗机器学习范式：(1)构造多模态选择题，其视觉组件可施加微妙的视觉扰动；(2)扰动被优化为使AI求解器被引导至特定错误答案；(3)诱导的答案模式形成统计指纹——盲目复制求解器答案的学生会比真实学生更频繁地重现该模式；(4)在现实黑箱助理假设下，使用可获取的替代模型（surrogate models）优化扰动，通过统计假设检验检测答案模式。

Result: 研究在三种主流多模态语言模型（Claude、Gemini、ChatGPT）上验证了该范式的可行性，证明对抗扰动能够诱导一致的响应模式，且这些模式能够通过统计假设检验实现原理性的检测，同时明确了该方法的潜力与局限性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"以机器自身的弱点对抗机器辅助推理"这一思路的可行性与边界，为教育评估的完整性保护提供了一种新的技术路径，但也揭示了该方法在实际部署中的约束条件。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI enables students to outsource cognitive effort to increasingly capable assistants, creating an illusion of competence while undermining the independent reasoning that education aims to cultivate. We investigate whether adversarial machine learning can be repurposed to protect educational exercises against such corrosive reliance. Our approach uses multimodal multiple-choice questions whose visual components can be protected with subtle visual perturbations that steer AI solvers toward designated incorrect answers. These responses form a statistical fingerprint: students who blindly copy a solver reproduce the induced answer pattern more frequently than genuine students. We study the feasibility of this paradigm under realistic black-box assistant assumptions using three of the most common state-of-the-art multimodal language models: Anthropic's Claude, Google's Gemini, and OpenAI's ChatGPT. By using accessible surrogate models, we optimize adversarial perturbations that induce consistent response patterns. Those patterns enable principled detection through statistical hypothesis testing. These findings establish both the promise and the limitations of fighting machine-assisted reasoning with the vulnerabilities of the machines themselves.

</details>


### [167] [MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01128)
*Yixin Zhang,Zhuohui Yao,Wenchi Cheng,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机辅助应急通信中的信息年龄（AoI）最小化问题，提出了一种自适应规则引导的多智能体深度强化学习框架MA-HEAD-Net，通过联合优化无人机轨迹控制、用户调度和检查点间隔选择，在动态应急场景中实现比现有基线更低的AoI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后场景中无人机对建立应急通信网络至关重要，而时效性救援任务对信息新鲜度（AoI）有极高要求，基于过时数据的决策可能导致无效的控制行动。因此需要最小化AoI来确保决策信息的时效性。

Method: 采用马尔可夫调制泊松过程建模突发性分组到达，利用有限块长理论捕捉传输时长、分组完成与AoI演化的耦合关系；提出小槽嵌入调度机制与自适应检查点间隔选择来平衡长包传输与短包响应；将无人机轨迹控制、用户调度和检查点间隔选择的联合优化建模为多智能体决策问题，开发了MA-HEAD-Net框架，将通信域规则先验引入门控多头策略中，在多头近端策略优化下联合优化策略与门控组件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，MA-HEAD-Net相较于代表性多智能体深度强化学习基线提升了策略形成效率，并在动态无人机应急通信场景中实现了比基于学习和启发式方法更低的AoI。

Conclusion: MA-HEAD-Net在动态无人机辅助应急通信场景中有效降低了AoI，验证了将通信领域规则先验融入多智能体强化学习框架能同时提升学习效率和系统性能。

Abstract: In post-disaster scenarios, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are critical for establishing emergency communication networks. For time-critical rescue missions, information freshness is crucial because decisions based on outdated data may lead to ineffective control actions. This paper investigates age of information (AoI) minimization for UAV-assisted emergency communications with heterogeneous emergency services. We model bursty packet arrivals using a Markov-modulated Poisson process and adopt finite blocklength theory to capture the coupling among transmission duration, packet completion, and AoI evolution. To balance delay-tolerant long-packet transmission and urgent short-packet response, we propose a mini-slot-embedded scheduling mechanism with adaptive checkpoint-interval selection. We formulate the joint optimization of UAV trajectory control, user scheduling, and checkpoint-interval selection as a multi-agent decision problem, and develop MA-HEAD-Net, an adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. MA-HEAD-Net incorporates communication-domain rule priors into a gated multi-head policy, where adaptive gates regulate the contributions of rule-prior and learned-policy logits for different subtasks. The policy and gating components are jointly optimized under multi-agent proximal policy optimization. Simulation results show that MA-HEAD-Net improves policy-formation efficiency compared with representative multi-agent deep reinforcement learning baselines and achieves lower AoI than both learning-based and heuristic methods in dynamic UAV-assisted emergency communication scenarios.

</details>


### [168] [PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01149)
*Xidong Yang,Xingyi Zhang,Wenhao Li,Wenyan Liu,Junjie Sheng,Yun Hua,Wei Yin,Tao Fang,Chuyun Shen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PATH-Bench基准，用于评估终身学习智能体的路径依赖行为，通过构造可控的正/负历史序列来测量前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘，并基于发现提出选择性经验使用（SEU）机制以提升终身智能体的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习智能体的评测基准很少考虑经验积累路径如何影响智能体的知识迁移与保留，缺乏对路径依赖效应的系统评估。

Method: PATH-Bench通过多模型上下文学习估计有向任务关系，构造以探针任务为中心的、具有受控帮助性和干扰性历史的序列，并在正/负主导历史下对探针任务进行重复评测，衡量平均性能、前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘。此外提出SEU（选择性经验使用）智能体外挂框架，动态调控路径积累的经验对每个新任务的影响。

Result: 在8个代表性智能体上对单轮代码生成和多轮工具使用任务进行评测，发现：经验效用同时取决于经验表示方式和任务交互结构；强迁移不保证强保留；后续经验可重塑早期获得的增益。SEU在多数设置下一致降低遗忘并提升前向迁移。

Conclusion: PATH-Bench提供了受控的评测框架和可操作的设计指导，表明设计更具选择性和鲁棒性的终身智能体需要同时考虑经验的表示形式、任务交互结构及经验积累路径中的时序效应。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly adapt through external learning states that store past interactions as retrievable memories or reusable skills, yet existing benchmarks rarely account for how the path of accumulated experience shapes what agents transfer and retain. In this work, we establish PATH-Bench, a benchmark for path-dependent evaluation of lifelong agents. PATH-Bench estimates directed task relationships via multi-model in-context learning, constructs probe-centered sequences with controlled helpful and interfering histories, and repeatedly evaluates probe tasks to measure average performance, forward transfer, backward transfer, and forgetting. We evaluate eight representative agents on single-turn code generation and multi-turn tool-use tasks under positive- and negative-dominant histories. Benchmark results show that experience utility depends jointly on how experience is represented and on the task's interaction structure, that strong transfer does not ensure retention, and that later experience can reshape gains acquired earlier in the learning path. Based on these findings, we propose Selective Experience Use (SEU), an agent harness that regulates how path-accumulated experience influences each new task, admitting helpful items while filtering out potential interference. SEU consistently reduces forgetting while improving forward transfer in the majority of settings. The PATH-Bench provides both a controlled evaluation framework and actionable guidance for designing more selective and robust lifelong agents.

</details>


### [169] [The Graph Language: How Knowledge Graphs Speak to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01175)
*Giuseppe Pirrò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRALAN通过可训练的语言中介生成保留图结构的关系令牌，使知识图谱能够直接在LLM的语义空间中"说话"，无需修改冻结的LLM即可实现KG-LLM集成，在复杂多跳推理问答任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力强但缺乏知识图谱提供的结构化知识支撑，而知识图谱与LLM的集成面临巨大挑战。现有方法难以在保持图结构完整性的同时充分利用LLM的推理能力。

Method: GRALAN引入可训练的语言中介（trainable language mediator），生成保留图结构的关系令牌（relational tokens），使知识图谱能够直接在LLM的语义空间中表达。对于问答任务，将问题重新框架为对问题聚焦子图（question-focused subgraphs）上的实体分类任务。

Result: 实验表明GRALAN显著优于现有方法，尤其在复杂的多跳推理任务上表现突出，验证了其结构保真度与LLM推理能力结合的有效性。

Conclusion: GRALAN建立了KG-LLM集成的新范式，通过保持结构保真度的同时利用LLM的推理能力，为知识密集型应用奠定了新基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at reasoning but benefit from grounding provided by Knowledge Graphs (KGs). However, integrating these paradigms is challenging. We introduce GRALAN, which enables KGs to speak directly in the LLM's semantic space through relational tokens that preserve graph structure. GRALAN-s trainable language mediator generates structured tokens for any frozen LLM, creating a foundation for knowledge-intensive applications. We demonstrate its effectiveness in question-answering by re-framing the task as entity classification over question-focused subgraphs. Experiments show that GRALAN significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly on complex multi-hop reasoning tasks, establishing a new paradigm for KG-LLM integration that maintains structural fidelity while leveraging LLMs' reasoning capabilities.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Truncated Differentiation for Inverse Potential MFGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00217)
*Siting Liu,Yat Tin Chow,Samy Wu Fung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究逆势平均场博弈（MFG）问题，从观测到的群体密度推断未知的空间逆向成本（流动性）映射。作者提出Jacobian-free反向传播方法（JFB-r），仅记录从分离热启动开始的最后r次迭代，并证明该方法在适度跟踪深度下能达到与完全展开相当的恢复精度，同时降低内存和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆势MFG是理解群体行为与成本结构关系的关键问题，但完全展开的前向求解器进行反向传播需要极高的内存和计算成本。作者旨在开发一种截断微分方法，在保持前向求解完整性的同时，仅对少量迭代进行反向跟踪，从而显著降低内存和运行时开销。

Method: 该方法包含两部分：(1) 使用预条件原始-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法求解前向MFG；(2) 开发Jacobian-free反向传播（JFB-r），从分离的热启动点开始，仅记录最后r次迭代用于反向传播，同时保留完整的前向求解。理论分析上，论文将精确近端对偶外推PDHG映射视为最大单调KKT算子的度量预解，利用这一预解视角证明JFB-r精确微分有限轨迹代理，并且在精确平衡分离点的局部固定活动集下，随着跟踪深度的增加收敛到隐式梯度。

Result: 在多个逆MFG设置下的数值实验中，JFB-r在适度的跟踪深度下可以达到与完全展开相当的恢复精度，同时显著减少内存和运行时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为逆MFG问题提供了一种高效且理论可证明的截断反向传播方法，在计算效率与恢复精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: We study inverse potential mean-field games (MFGs), in which an unknown spatial inverse-cost (mobility) map is inferred from observed population densities. We solve the forward MFG with a preconditioned primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method and develop Jacobian-free backpropagation (JFB-$r$), which records only the final $r$ iterations from a detached warm start while retaining the full forward solve. To analyze this truncated differentiation method, we show that the exact-proximal dual-extrapolated PDHG map is a metric resolvent of the maximal monotone KKT operator. This resolvent view shows that JFB-$r$ exactly differentiates a finite-trajectory surrogate and, under a locally fixed active set at an exact equilibrium detach point, converges to the implicit gradient as the tracked depth increases. Across several inverse-MFG settings, numerical experiments show that JFB-r at moderate tracked depths can achieve recovery accuracy comparable to full unrolling while reducing memory and runtime.

</details>


### [171] [Residual Centering and Error Bounds for Convex Approximation of Mixed-Integer Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00245)
*Alban Kryeziu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两阶段随机规划中混合整数追索函数的凸近似，通过坐标切片居中（coordinate slice-centering）条件和分部积分技巧，在有限不确定性箱上建立期望残差的确定性界（含各向异性全变差界和混合导数界），并针对精确居中与凸性不兼容的问题，提出基于交换坐标投影子的缺陷调整界（defect-adjusted bounds），提供可选择的误差证书和统一的一阶段决策质量保证；同时建立了锐利常数、非光滑密度扩展、张量积和几何推广，以及最大仿射凸拟合与审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段随机规划中，混合整数追索函数通常非凸，导致求解困难。凸近似是常见缓解手段，但如何量化凸近似与真实整数追索函数之间的期望残差（即一阶段决策质量的误差）缺乏系统性理论。现有方法缺少可验证的确定性界限，且精确居中条件可能与凸性冲突，需要更通用的误差分析框架。

Method: 针对凸近似残差的期望误差，在有限不确定性箱上提出坐标切片居中条件，利用分部积分导出各向异性全变差界和混合导数界（全坐标情形由密度的Vitali变差刻画）。当精确居中与凸性不兼容时（即使对全幺模天花板追索），引入交换坐标投影子将任意残差分解为居中分量和显式切片均值缺陷，构造缺陷调整界。此外推导锐利常数，将混合导数估计推广至非光滑密度，并发展张量积与几何扩展框架及最大仿射凸拟合和审计方法。

Result: 建立了凸近似期望误差的确定性上界，其核心条件为坐标切片居中；通过投影子分解得到缺陷调整界，可提供选择性误差证书和统一的一阶段决策质量保证；常数达到锐利（sharp），混合导数估计对非光滑密度依然成立，并给出了张量积、几何扩展下以及最大仿射凸拟合审计方法下的具体实现。

Conclusion: 本文为两阶段混合整数随机规划的凸近似误差提供了严格且可操作的理论保证，克服了精确居中与凸性的不兼容问题，使得误差上界在实践中可通过缺陷调整予以计算和验证，从而为一阶段决策质量提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We study convex approximations of mixed-integer recourse functions in two-stage stochastic programming. For first-stage decisions, the relevant approximation error is the signed expected residual between the convex approximation and the integer-recourse value function. On bounded uncertainty boxes, we identify residual conditions that yield deterministic bounds for this expectation error. The central condition is coordinate slice-centering, under which integration by parts gives an anisotropic total-variation bound and a mixed-derivative bound whose all-coordinate case is expressed through Vitali variation of the density. Exact centering, however, may be incompatible with convexity, even for totally unimodular ceiling recourse. We therefore use commuting coordinate projectors to decompose an arbitrary residual into a centered component and an explicit slice-mean defect. The resulting defect-adjusted bounds provide selectable error certificates and uniform first-stage decision-quality guarantees. We further establish sharp constants, extend the mixed-derivative estimate to nonsmooth densities, and develop tensor-product and geometric extensions of the framework, alongside a max-affine convex fitting and audit methodology.

</details>


### [172] [Persistent Mean Tracking in Client--Server Open Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00327)
*Amit Dutta,Fat-Hy Omar Rajab,Marcos M. Vasconcelos,Olugbenga M. Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究开放客户端-服务器网络中持久智能体的识别与均值跟踪问题。网络由频繁参与的持久智能体和仅零星出现的瞬态智能体组成。由于服务器只能观察到二进制活动指示符和标量更新，朴素平均无法恢复活跃持久均值。作者提出两阶段估计框架：第一阶段通过双层窗口识别程序分离持久与瞬态智能体，并给出显式有限窗界；第二阶段利用结构估计通过平滑滤波器跟踪活跃持久均值，证明跟踪误差几何收敛到由目标漂移和滤波器增益决定的邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放客户端-服务器网络中，智能体的参与模式是异构的：持久智能体频繁参与，而瞬态智能体仅零星出现。服务器只能看到二进制的活动指示符和来自活跃智能体的标量更新，这使得朴素平均无法恢复活跃持久均值，因为瞬态智能体的更新污染了聚合结果，且异构参与引入了偏差。需要在没有先验智能体类型信息的情况下，有效地识别持久智能体并跟踪其活跃均值。

Method: 提出两阶段估计框架。Phase I：设计双层基于窗口的识别程序——第一层形成单窗口活动决策，第二层跨窗口聚合这些决策以实现持久与瞬态智能体的高概率分离。在异构伯努利参与假设下，推导显式有限窗界，说明服务器需等待多少个窗口才能使分离以高概率发生。Phase II：利用得到的结构估计，通过平滑滤波器跟踪活跃持久均值，并分析跟踪误差的收敛性质。

Result: 在Phase I中获得了高概率分离的显式有限窗界；在Phase II中证明了跟踪误差以几何速率收敛到由目标漂移和滤波器增益决定的邻域。数值模拟验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架能够有效解决开放网络下持久智能体识别与均值跟踪问题，在瞬态智能体干扰和异构参与偏差存在的情况下，仍能实现可靠的结构识别和几何收敛的均值跟踪。

Abstract: We study persistent-agent identification and mean tracking in an open client-server network where agents participate intermittently. The network consists of a core of highly regular persistent agents and a subset of transient/non-persistent agents that appear only sporadically. Since the server observes only binary activity indicators and scalar updates from active agents, naive averaging cannot recover the active persistent mean because transient-agent updates contaminate the aggregate, and heterogeneous participation introduces bias. To address this, we propose a two-phase estimation framework. In Phase I, we develop a two-layer window-based identification procedure: the first layer forms single-window activity decisions, while the second aggregates these decisions across windows to create a high-probability separation between persistent and transient agents. Under heterogeneous Bernoulli participation, we derive explicit finite-window bounds that specify how many windows the server must wait for this separation to occur with high probability. In Phase II, we use the resulting structural estimate to track the active persistent mean via a smoothing filter, and show that the tracking error contracts geometrically to a neighborhood determined by the target drift and the filter gain. We provide numerical simulations to illustrate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [173] [Source Reliability Weighted Observer Design for Open Client Server Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00342)
*Amit Dutta,Marcos M. Vasconcelos,Olugbenga M. Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究开放客户端-服务器网络中的状态估计问题，提出了一种基于源可靠性加权的观测器。该观测器通过重复创新一致性（innovation consistency）学习每个活跃观测源的可靠性评分，并据此分配信息权重，从而在多源中包含无用（nuisance）源的场景下实现有限学习期后的准确状态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放客户端-服务器网络中，活跃观测源集合随时间变化，且活跃源不一定是潜在状态的有效传感器（可能包含干扰源）。服务器无法预先知道哪些代理提供有效的状态测量，因此需要一种能够自动识别可靠信息源并加权融合的状态估计方法。

Method: 提出源可靠性加权观测器（source-reliability-weighted observer）。状态估计采用标准固定先验加权最小二乘更新，但每个活跃观测所分配的信息量由源级可靠性评分决定，该评分通过重复创新一致性（repeated innovation consistency）在线学习得到。

Result: 数值结果表明：仅考虑所有活跃源的标准观测器存在有限偏差；而所提出的观测器先学习源可靠性，经过有限学习期后能够跟踪期望的潜在状态，消除了偏差。

Conclusion: 所提出的源可靠性加权观测器能够在存在未知干扰源和动态变化活跃源集合的开放网络环境中，通过在线学习源可靠性，实现有限学习期后的准确状态估计与跟踪。

Abstract: State estimation in open client server networks is challenging because the set of active observation sources changes over time, and active sources need not be valid sensors of the latent state. We study a client-server estimation problem in which a fixed but unknown state-consistent sensing class measures the latent state when active, while nuisance sources may arrive in the active set and generate observations from a different signal class. The server observes active sources, but it does not know which agents observe valid state measurements. We propose a source-reliability-weighted observer. The state estimate is a standard fixed-prior weighted least-squares update, but the information assigned to each active observation is determined by a source-level reliability score learned from repeated innovation consistency. Numerical results show that the observer, when all active sources are considered, has a finite bias, while the proposed observer initially learns source reliability and then tracks the desired latent state after a finite learning period.

</details>


### [174] [On the Iterate Convergence of AdaGrad for Generalized Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00482)
*Mathieu Besançon,Tung Quoc Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了AdaGrad算法族在凸可微目标函数上的序列收敛性结果，给出了三种主要变体(AdaNorm、AdaDiag、AdaFull)在凸且局部Lipschitz光滑目标下迭代收敛的充要条件，并在广义(L0,L1)光滑性及(L0,L1)多项式可修改光滑性假设下证明了更一般的收敛性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdaGrad算法族是广泛使用的自适应优化方法，但其迭代序列的收敛性(而非仅目标值收敛)在凸优化之外的条件下是否成立，此前在文献中是一个悬而未决的问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为AdaGrad在不同光滑性条件下的序列收敛提供严格的充要条件。

Method: (1) 针对凸且局部Lipschitz光滑的目标，推导了AdaGrad三种变体迭代序列收敛的充要条件；(2) 利用该通用结果，在广义(L0,L1)光滑性条件下研究三种变体，证明充分小常数步长下的序列收敛；(3) 在(L0,L1)多项式可修改光滑性假设(这是(L0,L1)广义光滑性的松弛，且被L-光滑函数和单变量多项式等广泛函数类满足)下，证明任意学习率下AdaGrad变体的序列收敛性；(4) 通过数值实验验证并构造反例。

Result: (1) 给出了凸且局部Lipschitz光滑目标下三种AdaGrad变体序列收敛的充要条件；(2) 在(L0,L1)广义光滑性条件下，证明了充分小常数步长的序列收敛；(3) 在(L0,L1)多项式可修改光滑性假设下，对任意学习率证明了序列收敛，揭示了AdaGrad的自适应性条件；(4) 数值实验构造了反例，表明仅光滑性不足以保证AdaGrad类算法的序列收敛，凸性或KL不等式等额外几何假设不可或缺。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了AdaGrad算法族序列收敛性的理论问题，明确了AdaGrad展现自适应性(即无需根据实例调参)所需的充分条件，并通过反例阐明了光滑性条件本身不足以保证迭代收敛，为自适应优化算法的收敛性理论提供了重要补充。

Abstract: We prove sequential convergence results for the AdaGrad algorithm family optimizing convex differentiable objectives. Specifically, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the convergence of iterates for the three main AdaGrad variants (AdaNorm, AdaDiag, AdaFull) when the objective is convex and locally Lipschitz-smooth, closing the question left open from the literature. We harness this general result to study the three variants under the generalized $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness condition and show sequential convergence for sufficiently small constant step size. Moreover, under the so-called $(L_0,L_1)$-polynomially modifiable smoothness assumption, which is a relaxation of the $(L_0,L_1)$ generalized smoothness property and is satisfied by many function classes such as $L$-smooth functions or univariate polynomials, sequential convergence for these AdaGrad variants is proved for arbitrary learning rates. This result provides conditions under which AdaGrad presents adaptivity, i.e., does not require tuning the parameters based on the instance. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations of the behavior of AdaGrad on convex and nonconvex functions. In particular, we construct a counterexample empirically showing that smoothness alone is not sufficient for the sequential convergence of AdaGrad-type algorithms, and suggesting that additional geometric hypotheses (e.g., convexity as in this paper, or the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz inequality) are indispensable for sequential convergence results.

</details>


### [175] [Quantitative Particle Approximation for Controlled Nonlinear Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00686)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Xihao He,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文估计了受控非线性滤波问题中粒子近似的值函数收敛速度。状态为平坦环面上的McKean-Vlasov扩散，条件滤波作为控制问题的状态变量，值函数求解Wasserstein空间上的二阶Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程。在数据光滑性、一致椭圆性和哈密顿量正则性假设下，建立了阶为N^{-1/6}（d=1）、N^{-1/6}(log N)^{1/3}（d=2）和N^{-1/(3d)}（d>2）的一致值函数误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控非线性滤波问题在随机控制与统计推断中具有广泛的应用背景，但其值函数在Wasserstein空间上求解二阶HJB方程在数值上极具挑战。通过N粒子系统近似原始控制的McKean-Vlasov问题是一个自然的数值策略，但需要严格的理论保证来衡量近似精度，尤其是在非可分离奖励和受控漂移的通用框架下。

Method: 采用集中式N粒子控制问题来近似原始问题，其上施加单一控制于整个群体。证明结合了四个关键技术：公共噪声方向上的平移提升（translation lift）、Fourier-Wasserstein上下卷积（inf- and sup-convolutions）、粘性比较原理（viscosity comparison），以及关于N一致（uniform in N）的粒子导数估计。

Result: 建立了对维度d依赖的一致值函数误差界：d=1时为N^{-1/6}，d=2时为N^{-1/6}(log N)^{1/3}，d>2时为N^{-1/(3d)}。这些收敛速度在不同维度下均显式给出。

Conclusion: 该工作为受控非线性滤波问题提供了首个明确的粒子近似收敛率，通过创新的分析工具组合（平移提升、Fourier-Wasserstein卷积与粘性比较）在Wasserstein空间上实现了对N粒子系统中一致误差的控制，为相关数值方法的理论保障奠定了基础。

Abstract: We estimate convergence rates of value functions for particle approximations of a controlled nonlinear filtering problem. The state is a McKean--Vlasov diffusion on the flat torus, driven by hidden idiosyncratic noise and observed common noise. The filter---the conditional law of the state given the observations---serves as the state variable of the control problem, and the associated value function solves a second-order Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equation on the Wasserstein space. We approximate this problem by a centralized \(N\)-particle control problem with independent idiosyncratic noises and a common observation noise. The framework accommodates nonseparable rewards and controlled drifts. Since a single control is applied to the entire population, the Hamiltonian is defined by an optimization performed after integration over the population. Under smoothness of the data, uniform ellipticity, and regularity of this Hamiltonian, we establish uniform value-function error bounds of order \(N^{-1/6}\) for \(d=1\), \(N^{-1/6}(\log N)^{1/3}\) for \(d=2\), and \(N^{-1/(3d)}\) for \(d>2\). The proof combines a translation lift in the common-noise direction, Fourier--Wasserstein inf- and sup-convolutions, viscosity comparison, and particle derivative estimates uniform in \(N\).

</details>


### [176] [Dynamic-Threshold Algorithms for the Continuous Quadratic Knapsack Problem: Reset Mechanisms and Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00740)
*Yong-Jin Liu,Peicheng Xie,Chuan Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带权等式约束的连续二次背包问题，提出了一种动态阈值算法（DTA）及其无重置变体NDTA。DTA通过加法、重置和删除三种操作维持阈值不变量，并证明了有限终止性和正确性。研究分析了重置操作的理论作用，构造了DTA线性时间而NDTA二次时间的实例，并在实验中验证了两种算法在千万级变量规模下近似线性的经验复杂度，显著优于多种现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Condat算法是高效的动态阈值单纯形投影方法，但其在带权等式约束下的一般化扩展，以及重置（reset）和删除（removal）操作在算法中的理论作用尚未得到充分分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，深入理解动态阈值机制在更一般问题上的行为，并为重置操作的必要性提供理论刻画。

Method: 提出了动态阈值算法（DTA），通过加法（addition）、重置（reset）和删除（removal）三种操作维护阈值不变量来求解带权等式约束的连续二次背包问题。文中证明了算法的有限终止性和正确性，推导了一个保证重置不会发生的充分条件，据此设计了无重置变体NDTA。通过实例构造对比分析DTA与NDTA的复杂度差异，并进行大规模数值实验与多种现有算法进行性能对比。

Result: 理论方面，证明了DTA的正确性和有限终止性；构造了DTA为Θ(n)时间而NDTA为Θ(n²)时间的实例；给出了当权重比和每次删除遍历的删除数有界时，正阈值增量所需的线性次遍历的一个下界条件（最小非零间隔与数据范围之比至多为exp[-Θ(n log n)]）。实验方面，在10^7变量规模下，DTA和NDTA展示了近似线性的经验复杂度，并在运行时间上优于Secant、WMVA、Variable Fixing、Newton、Median Search、Heap和Sort等方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明动态阈值算法能够有效扩展到带权等式约束的连续二次背包问题，重置操作虽然在理论上有助于实现线性时间实例，但在实践中两种变体的经验性能均接近线性且优于现有方法，为实际应用提供了高效且理论上可保证的求解方案。

Abstract: Condat's algorithm is an efficient dynamic-threshold method for projection onto the simplex, but its extension to weighted equality constraints and the algorithmic roles of resetting and removal have received limited analysis. We develop a dynamic-threshold algorithm (DTA) for a continuous quadratic knapsack problem with a weighted equality constraint. DTA maintains a threshold invariant through three operations--addition, reset, and removal--and we establish its finite termination and correctness. A sufficient condition under which reset cannot occur motivates a simpler no-reset variant, NDTA. We construct instances for which DTA runs in $Θ (n)$ time whereas NDTA requires $Θ(n^2)$ time, although both algorithms have quadratic worst-case complexity. We further show that, when the weight ratio and the number of deletions per removal pass are bounded, a linear number of passes with positive threshold increments requires the minimum nonzero gap between input values, normalized by the data range, to be at most $\exp [- Θ(n \log n)]$. Numerical experiments with up to 10^7 variables demonstrate that DTA and NDTA achieve approximately linear empirical scaling, and outperform Secant, WMVA, Variable Fixing, Newton, Median Search, Heap, and Sort in running time.

</details>


### [177] [Learning-Based Stochastic Optimal Control with Infinite-Horizon Probabilistic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01151)
*Francesco Cordiano,Kanghui He,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有无限时域联合机会约束的随机最优控制问题，通过状态增广将其转化为受限马尔可夫决策过程，证明强对偶性后利用拉格朗日对偶框架转化为无约束问题，并提出对偶上升算法收敛到最优且可行的确定性马尔可夫策略，同时设计离线学习的价值函数逼近算法以降低在线控制计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限时域联合机会约束下的随机最优控制问题在实际工程中广泛存在，但求解难度大。联合机会约束（多个约束同时以一定概率满足）使得问题具有非凸性和时间耦合性，传统的在线预测控制方法计算开销大，需要寻找更高效且具有理论保证的求解框架。

Method: 首先通过状态增广将原问题重构为受限马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），使得代价和约束函数均具有加性结构；随后证明此公式满足强对偶性，从而通过拉格朗日对偶框架转化为等价的无约束问题；提出对偶上升算法迭代求解，并证明其收敛到增广状态空间上的最优且可行的确定性马尔可夫策略；最后针对连续状态-输入空间，提出离线训练的专用学习算法来逼近价值函数，降低在线控制阶段的计算复杂度。

Result: 在数值示例中验证了所提方法的有效性，与在线预测控制方法相比，在控制性能相当的情况下，显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种兼具理论保证和计算效率的无限时域联合机会约束随机最优控制求解框架，通过强对偶性实现问题解耦，结合离线学习进一步提升了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider stochastic optimal control problems with infinite-horizon joint chance constraints. By means of an appropriate state augmentation, we reformulate the original problem as a constrained Markov decision process, in which both the cost and the constraint function exhibit an additive structure. We then prove that this formulation enjoys strong duality, thereby enabling us to reformulate the problem as an equivalent unconstrained one in the Lagrange dual framework. We propose a dual-ascent algorithm to solve the resulting problem and show that it converges to a deterministic Markov policy defined over the augmented state space that is both optimal and feasible. To accommodate continuous state-input spaces, we propose a dedicated learning algorithm to approximate the value function in an offline training setting, thereby significantly reducing the computational complexity of the online control phase. We then test our approach on a numerical example and demonstrate its effectiveness compared to online predictive control methods in terms of performance and computational complexity.

</details>


### [178] [Revisiting Decomposition-Invariant Conditional Gradient Methods for Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01243)
*Dan Garber*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了分解不变条件梯度方法，用于在多面体上最小化凸β-光滑函数。针对2层多面体设计了无参数dyadic步长规则，获得与最优面的维度（而非环境维度）相关的线性收敛率；针对一般多面体，在面部二次增长条件下，提出了迭代复杂度为n + βD²/(αr*²) + (d*+1)βD²/(α_F)log(1/ε)的参数无关方法，显著优于以往条件梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于away-step的条件梯度方法在多面体优化中收敛率依赖环境维度n，这在高维问题中效率较低。作者希望设计一种收敛率仅依赖于最优面的维度d*而非环境维度n的方法，从而在最优面为低维时获得显著加速。

Method: 对于2级多面体，设计简单且参数无关的dyadic步长规则，在α-二次增长条件下获得线性收敛率。对于一般多面体，在更强的α_F-面部二次增长条件下，引入一种仅依赖标准线搜索计算、参数无关的分解不变条件梯度方法。

Result: 一般多面体的迭代复杂度为n + βD²/(αr*²) + (d*+1)βD²/(α_F)·log(1/ε)，其中d*为最优面维度，r*为最优集与不含最优解的面之间的分离参数。在max{α/α_F(d*+1), 1/r*²} ≪ n的合理工况下，该结果优于以往需βD²n/α·log(1/ε)复杂度的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在最优面为低维且分离参数合适时，能显著优于标准away-step条件梯度方法，实现了收敛率从环境维度n到最优面维度d*的转化，且保持参数无关性与仅需标准线搜索的实现简便性。

Abstract: We revisit Decomposition-Invariant Conditional Gradient methods, originally introduced by Garber and Meshi in 2016, for minimizing a convex and $β$-smooth function over a polytope in $\reals^n$, under an $α$-quadratic growth condition. For 2-level polytopes we design a simple and parameter-free dyadic step-size rule that yields a linear convergence rate which scales with the dimension of the optimal face and not with the ambient dimension as in standard away-step-based conditional gradient methods for polytopes. For general polytopes, under a slightly stronger condition of $α_{\mathrm{F}}$-\textit{facial quadratic growth}, we introduce a method whose number of iterations to reach an $ε$-approximate solution is of the order $n+\frac{βD^2}{αr^{*2}} + \frac{(d^*+1)βD^2}{α_{\mathrm{F}}}\log(1/ε)$, where $d^*$ is the dimension of the optimal face, $r^*$ is a separation parameter between the optimal set and faces that do not contain an optimal solution, and $D$ is the diameter of the polytope. This method is also parameter-free and only relies on standard line-search computations. The second result improves upon previous conditional gradient methods, whose number of iterations to $ε$-approximation scales with $\frac{βD^2n}α\log(1/ε)$, in a meaningful regime $\max\{\fracα{α_{\rm F}}(d^*+1), \frac{1}{r^{*2}}\} \ll n$

</details>


### [179] [Rake-Compress Riccati Recursions for Parallel Scenario-Tree Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01332)
*João Sousa-Pinto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于rake-compress树收缩的代数精确并行求解器，用于场景树模型预测控制（MPC）中的最优控制问题，将标准树Riccati递归的串行依赖链从O(树高)缩短到O(log N) span，同时保持O(N)的复杂度和存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景树MPC的数值求解通常依赖于分支LQR子问题的序列求解，标准的树Riccati递归虽然具有线性计算复杂度，但其依赖链与树的高度成正比，限制了并行性。随着场景树规模增大，需要一种既能保持线性运算次数又能大幅提升并行度的求解方法。

Method: 基于rake-compress树收缩的并行求解器：先对各节点进行独立的局部控制凝聚；rake操作消除叶节点及其父边，将缩减贡献并入父节点数据；compress操作消除一元节点并将两个相邻边合并为一条边，利用与链式并行Riccati方法相同的条件值组合。通过双向收缩将任意有根树收缩到根节点，再反向恢复所有Riccati系数、状态、控制和乘子。支持半正定对偶正则化（含无正则化情况），并提供精确的线性规模提升以覆盖标准场景MPC中每个信息节点一个控制变量的约定。

Result: 给定可复用的拓扑规划，对于N个节点、固定状态和控制维度的系统，算法具有O(N)算术复杂度和存储、O(log N) span，与树的高度、平衡性和最大出度无关。证明了收缩恒等式及与KKT系统的等价性。发布了三个MIT许可的JAX包：双向收缩、对偶正则化LQR求解器，以及面向用户的树结构最优控制原始-对偶内点求解器。

Conclusion: 该工作为场景树MPC提供了一个理论上并行效率最优的精确求解框架，通过代数恒等变形将树的拓扑并行性充分挖掘，在不需要任何数值近似的前提下获得对数级的并行span，是树结构最优控制数值方法的重要进展。

Abstract: Scenario-tree model predictive control (MPC) represents future information by a rooted tree and optimizes a nonanticipative policy over that tree. Numerical methods for solving the resulting nonlinear program typically compute their search directions through a sequence of branched linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) subproblems. The standard tree Riccati recursion requires linear work but has a dependency chain proportional to tree height.
  We present an algebraically exact parallel solver based on rake-compress tree contraction. After independent local control condensation, its two operations act on node and edge data that represent conditional quadratic functions. A rake eliminates a leaf and its parent edge, adding their reduced contribution to the parent-node data. A compress eliminates a unary node and replaces its two adjacent edges by one edge, using the same conditional-value composition as parallel Riccati methods on a chain. Together they contract an arbitrary rooted tree to its root; reversing the contraction recovers every Riccati coefficient, state, control, and multiplier. Given a reusable topology plan, a solve with $N$ nodes and fixed state and control dimensions has $O(N)$ arithmetic work and storage and $O(\log N)$ span, independently of tree height, balance, and maximum out-degree.
  The formulation allows positive-semidefinite dual regularization, including the unregularized case, and an exact linear-size lifting covers the standard scenario-MPC convention of one control per information node. We prove the contraction identities and equivalence to the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) system. Three MIT-licensed JAX packages implement the bidirectional contraction, the dual-regularized LQR solver, and a user-facing primal-dual interior-point solver for tree-structured optimal control.

</details>


### [180] [A Geometric Inverse Source Problem for Stochastic Parabolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01351)
*Yunzhang Li,Qi Lü,Meizhi Qian,Yu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机抛物方程中同时重构漂移项和扩散项两个未知确定性源的支撑集的几何逆问题。利用部分边界通量测量唯一确定两个源支撑，证明了带周长正则化的目标泛函最小元的存在性，推导了Hadamard型边界形状导数公式，并据此开发了形状梯度重构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机偏微分方程（SPDE）框架下，同时恢复出现在漂移项和扩散项中的两个源支撑是一个具有挑战性的几何逆问题。此前尚无关于同时恢复两类源支撑的形状导数公式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过部分边界通量测量建立唯一性；对带周长正则化的目标泛函证明最小元存在性；对于光滑界面，推导Hadamard型边界形状导数公式，揭示随机双通道结构——伴随态控制漂移源界面灵敏度，鞅分量控制扩散源界面灵敏度；基于该公式开发形状梯度重构方法。

Result: 数值实验表明该形状梯度重构方法在部分和全部边界观测下均能有效区分两个源通道。这是首次在SPDE设置中获得同时恢复漂移源支撑和扩散源支撑的Hadamard型形状导数公式。

Conclusion: 本文首次在随机抛物方程中建立了同时恢复漂移源和扩散源支撑的形状导数公式，并通过数值实验验证了所提出的形状梯度重构方法的有效性，为SPDE框架下的多源几何逆问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper addresses the geometric inverse problem of simultaneously recovering two unknown deterministic source supports in a stochastic parabolic equation, where one source appears in the drift term and the other in the diffusion term. We establish that partial boundary flux measurements alone uniquely determine both supports. Moreover, we prove the existence of minimizers for a perimeter-regularized objective functional. For smooth interfaces, we derive a Hadamard-type boundary representation of the shape derivative. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first such formula for the simultaneous recovery of a drift-source support and a diffusion-source support in an SPDE setting. The derivative exhibits a genuinely stochastic two-channel structure: the adjoint state governs the sensitivity of the drift-source interface, while the martingale component controls the sensitivity of the diffusion-source interface. Based on this formula, we develop a shape-gradient reconstruction method. Numerical experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and its capacity to distinguish between the two source channels under both full and partial boundary observations.

</details>


### [181] [Coordinate Optimality Reformulation for Mixed-Integer Convex Programs with Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01385)
*Tong Xu,Salar Fattahi,Andrés Gómez,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出坐标最优性重构（CORe）框架，通过将坐标级最优性信息纳入标准指示变量混合整数凸优化模型中，在保持全局最优性的同时显著提升分支定界求解性能，尤其适用于稀疏和结构化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准big-M形式的指示变量混合整数凸优化方法在求解稀疏或结构化问题时，由于分支定界搜索空间巨大且缺乏对坐标级结构的利用，求解效率低下。作者希望通过引入坐标级最优性条件来暴露可被利用的问题结构，从而提升求解性能。

Method: CORe框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 坐标级最优性条件的推导；(2) 闭式解的刻画；以及(3) 析取重构。通过这些组件将坐标最优性信息增强到标准指示变量公式化中，同时确保全局最优性不被破坏。

Result: 在多个问题族（包括二次规划和鲁棒单指标模型）的计算实验中，CORe相比标准big-M公式化在分支定界求解器性能上获得了显著提升。

Conclusion: CORe是一种有效且通用性强的重构框架，通过利用坐标级最优性信息增强指示变量混合整数凸优化模型的求解效率，尤其在稀疏和结构化场景下优势明显。

Abstract: We consider mixed-integer convex optimization problems in which binary indicators control continuous variables. We introduce the \emph{Coordinate Optimality Reformulation} (CORe) framework, which augments standard indicator formulations by incorporating coordinate-wise optimality information. The resulting reformulations preserve global optimality while substantially improving branch-and-bound performance, particularly in sparse and structured settings where the coordinate-wise optimality conditions expose exploitable problem structure. We first develop the main components of CORe, including coordinate-wise optimality conditions, closed-form characterizations, and disjunctive reformulations. We then demonstrate the framework across multiple problem families, including quadratic problems and robust single-index models. Computational experiments show that CORe can substantially improve solver performance compared with standard big-$M$ formulations.

</details>


### [182] [Universal Triangle Covering Curve and Polygonal Chain: Escaping Forest and Fitting Worm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01393)
*Zhipeng Deng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架来解决用三角形覆盖曲线/多边形链以及将曲线拟合到三角形中的问题，将这些几何问题归约为Bellman's lost-in-a-forest问题和Moser's worm问题的特例，通过保持曲线静止而让三角形平移旋转的建模方式，推导出带支撑函数约束的泛函极小化公式，并证明了公式的等价性与收敛性，最后给出数值结果和推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖曲线和多边形链的三角形问题以及将曲线拟合进三角形的问题在几何优化和计算几何中具有基础重要性，但缺乏统一的数学框架来形式化求解。作者试图将这些看似不同的问题统一到Bellman's lost-in-a-forest问题（逃出三角森林）和Moser's worm问题（被三角形覆盖）的框架下，建立一套一般性的数学公式和求解方法。

Method: 作者将问题重新建模为保持曲线固定而允许三角形做平移和旋转运动的形式，从而将几何覆盖问题转化为一个最优化问题。在此基础上推导出带有支撑函数（support function）约束的泛函极小化（functional minimization）公式来求解该问题，并证明了所提出公式的等价性和收敛性。最后采用数值方法对任意角度三角形的曲线覆盖进行了求解。

Result: 通过数值方法获得了用各种角度任意三角形覆盖曲线的结果，并进一步推导出若干推论和变体结果，包括闭曲线和闭多边形链的情况，验证了所提公式的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了覆盖曲线/多边形链与三角形这一类几何优化问题的统一泛函极小化框架，理论上证明了公式的等价性与收敛性，数值上验证了方法的可行性，并为闭曲线等扩展情形提供了推论。后续可推广到更多形状和更高维度的覆盖问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a general formulation to address the problems of covering curves and polygonal chains with triangle, and fitting these curves into triangle. These problems can be formulated as special cases of Bellman's lost-in-a-forest problem (escaping triangular forest) and Moser's worm problem (covered by triangle). We model and reformulate the problem by keeping the curve stationary while allowing the triangle to translate and rotate. Subsequently, we derive the functional minimization formulation with support function constraints to solve. We also prove the equivalence and convergence of the formulas. Finally, we employ numerical methods and present results for covering curves with arbitrary triangles of various angles. We also present some corollaries and variant results, including closed curves and closed polygonal chains.

</details>


### [183] [Generalized Quadratic Gradient: A New Direction in Optimization via the Fusion of Positive-Definite Curvature Matrices and Gradients into A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01552)
*John Chiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出广义二次梯度（GQG）框架，将二次梯度原理推广到更广泛的牛顿类优化算法中，证明其构建核心要求可泛化到任意满足局部二次模型平稳条件的正定曲率矩阵，超越传统的常Hessian、对角近似或BFGS代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二次梯度方法（QG、SQG、QQG）分别依赖特定的Hessian近似方式（如常数矩阵、对角近似或BFGS），缺乏统一的理论框架，限制了曲率感知优化算法的发展空间。作者希望抽象出这些方法的共同结构，为更广泛的牛顿类优化算法提供理论基础。

Method: 通过抽象现有二次梯度方法（QG、SQG、QQG）的共同结构，提出GQG统一框架。核心论证是二次梯度构建的基本要求可泛化为：任意满足局部二次模型平稳条件的正定曲率矩阵均可用于构建二次梯度，不限于特定的Hessian近似形式。在此基础上，探索除BFGS之外的各种正定Hessian代理来构建广义二次梯度。

Result: 论文从理论层面证明了二次梯度原理的通用性，揭示了其核心要求（正定曲率矩阵满足局部二次模型平稳条件）并不依赖特定Hessian近似，为使用各种正定Hessian代理（超出BFGS）构建二次梯度提供了理论依据和更广泛的研究基础。

Conclusion: GQG提供了一个统一且扩展的框架，将二次梯度原理推广到更广泛的牛顿类优化算法，打破了此前对特定Hessian近似的依赖限制，为开发和设计新的曲率感知优化算法开辟了更广阔的空间。

Abstract: Quadratic Gradient (QG) is a Newton-type optimization framework that bridges first-order gradient descent and second-order optimization by incorporating curvature information into gradient updates. Simplified Quadratic Gradient (SQG) reduces the complexity of QG construction while preserving its optimization capability, whereas Quasi-Quadratic Gradient (QQG) extends the quadratic gradient principle to quasi-Newton methods such as BFGS.
  In this paper, we propose **Generalized Quadratic Gradient (GQG)**, a unified framework that extends the quadratic gradient principle to a broader class of Newton-type optimization algorithms. By abstracting the common structure of existing quadratic gradient methods, we show that the fundamental requirement of quadratic gradient construction is not limited to specific Hessian approximations, such as constant Hessian matrices, diagonal Hessian approximations, or BFGS-based Hessian surrogates. Instead, it can be generalized to any positive-definite curvature matrix satisfying the stationary condition of a local quadratic model.
  Based on this perspective, we investigate the construction of generalized quadratic gradients using various positive-definite Hessian surrogates beyond BFGS, providing a broader foundation for developing curvature-aware optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [184] [Convergence and Stability Analysis of a Generalized Proximal Point Algorithm and Its Inexact Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01584)
*Ba Khiet Le,Boris S. Mordukhovich,Michel A. Thera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文引入了自适应强单调性的概念，并证明了广义近端点算法（GPPA）在希尔伯特空间中求解非单调包含问题近似解时的线性收敛性，同时研究了存在非递减误差的精确广义近端点算法（IGPPA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端点算法是求解包含问题的重要方法，但传统的单调性假设限制了其应用范围。本文旨在通过引入自适应强单调性的新概念，放宽对单调性的严格要求，从而推广近端点算法的适用范围并建立收敛保证。

Method: 引入自适应强单调性的新概念，基于此建立GPPA在非单调包含问题中的线性收敛结果。对于IGPPA，在允许非递减误差存在的情况下分析其收敛行为，扩展了传统精确算法对误差的严格限制。

Result: 证明了GPPA在自适应强单调性条件下对非单调包含问题具有线性收敛性，并分析了IGPPA在非递减误差情形下的收敛性质。

Conclusion: 通过引入自适应强单调性，成功将广义近端点算法的收敛性分析从单调问题推广到非单调问题，并且不完全（不精确）版本在实际误差干扰下仍保持良好的收敛表现。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the notion of adaptive strong monotonicity and establish a linear convergence of the recently proposed Generalized Proximal Point Algorithm (GPPA) for computing approximate solutions to nonmonotone inclusion problems in Hilbert spaces. We also investigate the Inexact Generalized Proximal Point Algorithm (IGPPA) in the presence of nondiminishing errors.

</details>


### [185] [Non-KKT Accumulation in Entropic Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01658)
*Kuangyu Ding,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个反直觉的结论：对于由Legendre核生成的镜像下降算法，即使步长选取恰当、目标值单调不增，有界镜像下降序列的累积点也未必是KKT驻点。作者通过构造光滑目标函数和Shannon熵镜像下降在非负象限（n≥3）及概率单纯形（n≥4）上的反例，得到累积点集合为光滑边界圆，其中包含一非空相对开弧由非KKT点组成。这是首个具有非增目标值的有界镜像下降序列的KKT累积反例，表明病态源于Bregman几何在边界处的退化而非下降失败或步长不当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像下降算法是优化领域的基础方法，其收敛性分析中一个最基本且长期悬而未决的问题是：在适当步长下，有界镜像下降序列的每个累积点是否必然是KKT驻点？此前已有障碍在于Legendre梯度的边界爆炸效应，使得分析该问题极其困难。本文的目标是正面回答这一问题并揭示其本质。

Method: 作者构造了C^∞光滑目标函数和有界镜像下降序列。具体地，在非负象限R_+^n（n≥3）和概率单纯形Δ_n（n≥4）上使用Shannon熵作为Legendre核生成镜像下降。构造的关键在于利用边界处逆熵度量在活跃坐标上的消失效应，从而使累积点集合成为光滑边界圆，且该圆上存在非空相对开弧由非KKT点组成。步长取α_k ~ k^{-β}（β∈(1/2,1)），目标值非增，且目标函数满足熵相对光滑条件。

Result: 成功构造出满足所有标准条件（适当步长、目标非增、光滑目标函数、熵相对光滑）的镜像下降序列，其累积点集合中出现非KKT点。这是首个此类反例，彻底否定了“有界镜像下降序列的累积点必为KKT驻点”这一猜想。

Conclusion: 该反例揭示了镜像下降的病态现象根源在于Bregman几何在可行域边界处的退化性（熵度量在活跃坐标上消失），而非算法下降失败或步长选取不当。这为理解镜像下降算法的极限行为提供了重要的理论洞见，并提示需要重新审视该算法在边界附近的收敛性质。

Abstract: For mirror descent generated by a Legendre kernel, perhaps one of the most basic question in optimization is this: must every accumulation point of a bounded mirror descent sequence be Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) stationary under proper stepsizes? We show that the answer is no. A longstanding obstacle to resolving this question is the boundary blow-up of the Legendre gradient: it keeps every mirror step in the interior, while at a boundary limit, the inverse entropy metric vanishes on active coordinates and can erase the dual-feasibility in the KKT system. We construct $C^\infty$ objectives and bounded sequences generated by the Shannon-entropic mirror descent on the nonnegative orthant $\R_+^n$, for every $n\geq 3$, and on the probability simplex $Δ_n$, for every $n\geq 4$, such that, in each case, the set of accumulation points is a smooth boundary circle containing a nonempty relatively open arc of non-KKT points. The steps satisfy $α_k\asymp k^{-β}$ with $β\in(1/2,1)$, the objective values are nonincreasing, and the objectives are entropy-relatively smooth. Hence the pathology stems from the degeneracy of the Bregman geometry at the boundary, rather than from failure of descent, or improper stepsizes. To the best of our knowledge, these provide the first counterexamples to KKT accumulation for bounded mirror descent sequences with nonincreasing objective values.

</details>


### [186] [A noncommunicative Kalman condition for null controllability of backward stochastic parabolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01836)
*Mohamed Fadili*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有常数确定性耦合矩阵、标量空间扩散和局部化漂移控制的n维倒向随机热方程系统，建立了零能控性的充要代数条件：控制矩阵的列必须在两个耦合矩阵的联合非交换作用下生成状态空间。通过分离随机内部动力学与标量热半群，证明有限维随机Gramian在联合词秩条件下是正定的，并满足多项式小时间强制估计，结合Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式和高频耗散得到对偶前向系统的可观测性，从而导出零能控性；该条件也是必要的，其失效将产生非平凡不变不可观子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究倒向随机热方程系统的零能控性问题，特别是耦合项同时作用于第一状态和鞅被积函数的复杂情形，旨在建立精确的代数判据来刻画系统可控性，并给出严格的理论证明。

Method: 首先将随机内部动力学与标量热半群分离；然后构造有限维随机Gramian，证明其在联合词秩条件下为正定并满足多项式小时间强制估计；结合Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式与高频耗散，证明对偶前向系统的可观测性，从而推导零能控性；最后通过反例构造不变不可观子空间证明条件的必要性。

Result: 建立了零能控性的充要条件：控制矩阵的列向量在联合非交换耦合矩阵作用下生成的线性空间中必须张满整个状态空间。该代数条件同时保证了随机Gramian的正定性和对偶系统的可观测性，且条件失效时系统不可控。

Conclusion: 为倒向随机热方程耦合系统的零能控性提供了完整的代数刻画，该联合词秩条件既是充分条件也是必要条件，揭示了确定性耦合结构与随机能控性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study a system of $n$ backward stochastic heat equations with constant deterministic coupling matrices, scalar spatial diffusion, and a localized drift control. The coupling acts both on the first state and on the martingale integrand. We establish a necessary and sufficient algebraic condition for null controllability: the columns of the control matrix must generate the state space under the joint noncommutative action of the two coupling matrices. The proof separates the stochastic internal dynamics from the scalar heat semigroup. A finite-dimensional stochastic Gramian is shown to be positive exactly under the joint word-rank condition and to satisfy a polynomial small-time coercivity estimate. Combined with the Lebeau--Robbiano spectral inequality and high-frequency dissipation, this yields observability of the dual forward system and hence null controllability. The condition is also necessary, as its failure produces a nontrivial invariant unobservable subspace.

</details>


### [187] [Sparse Linear Surrogates for Interpretable Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01858)
*Marc Goerigk,Michael Hartisch,Sebastian Merten*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了预算分配问题的线性替代模型，通过稀疏线性规则将实例映射到基于特征的解的表示，并分别给出了基于混合整数规划的精确方法和启发式方法，通过计算实验验证了两种方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法缺乏内在可解释性，难以满足对可解释优化方法的需求，因此需要开发本质上可解释的优化方法。

Method: 提出了用于预算分配问题的稀疏线性替代模型，将实例映射为基于特征的解的表示；同时开发了基于混合整数规划的精确方法和一种启发式算法来计算这些替代模型。

Result: 通过计算实验对精确方法和启发式方法的性能进行了分析比较。

Conclusion: 提出的线性替代模型为预算分配问题提供了一种可解释的求解途径，精确方法与启发式方法的结合兼顾了最优性与计算效率。

Abstract: To address the demand for inherently interpretable optimization methods, we introduce novel linear surrogates for budget allocation problems. These surrogates consist of sparse linear rules that map instances to feature-based representations of solutions. We present an exact approach based on mixed-integer programming as well as a heuristic for their computation. The performance of both approaches is analyzed through computational experiments.

</details>


### [188] [Safe screening rules for portfolio optimization with linear and cardinality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01871)
*Nanari Wada,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano,Jun-ya Gotoh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基数约束投资组合优化问题的安全筛选（safe screening）规则，通过透视松弛和Fenchel对偶推导出资产特定分数，在优化前安全固定二元选股变量为0或1，在不牺牲全局最优性的前提下大幅降低问题规模。实验表明在S&P 500和Russell 2000数据集上显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基数约束（限制持有资产数量）在实际投资组合优化中至关重要，可降低监控和交易成本，但该问题为NP-hard，随着候选资产规模增大，全局求解的计算难度急剧上升。现有的安全筛选技术尚无法直接应用于此类问题，需要一种能在优化前安全固定决策变量、同时保留全局最优性保证的方法。

Method: 利用L2正则化项的透视松弛（perspective relaxation）和Fenchel对偶理论，推导出融入线性约束Lagrange乘子的资产特定分数。结合基于松弛的下界和可行解的上界，这些分数可将二元资产选择变量安全地固定为0或1，从而在不排除任何全局最优解的前提下缩减问题规模。

Result: 在S&P 500和Russell 2000数据集上的实验表明，该方法在挑战性场景下（特别是中等或较强正则化以及较低的收益要求条件下）实现了显著的计算性能提升，验证了安全筛选作为保持最优性的预处理技术在大规模基数约束投资组合优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 安全筛选是一种保持最优性的预处理技术，能够在不牺牲全局最优性的前提下大幅提升大规模基数约束投资组合优化的计算效率，为解决此类NP-hard问题提供了实用的加速手段。

Abstract: In portfolio optimization, a cardinality constraint, which limits the number of assets held, plays a key role in cutting down monitoring and transaction costs. However, the resulting problem is NP-hard and becomes computationally difficult to solve globally as the number of candidate assets grows. Safe screening addresses this difficulty by fixing decision variables before optimization without excluding any globally optimal solution, thereby reducing the problem size while preserving optimality guarantees. We propose safe screening rules for cardinality-constrained portfolio optimization with a convex quadratic objective function and linear constraints. Using a perspective relaxation of the L2-regularization term and Fenchel duality, we derive asset-specific scores that incorporate the Lagrange multipliers of the linear constraints. Combined with a relaxation-based lower bound and a feasible-solution upper bound, these scores safely fix binary asset-selection variables to zero or one. Experiments on S&P 500 and Russell 2000 datasets show substantial computational improvements on challenging cases, particularly under moderate or strong regularization and less stringent return requirements. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of safe screening as an optimality-preserving preprocessing technique that greatly boosts computational efficiency in large-scale cardinality-constrained portfolio optimization.

</details>


### [189] [A Continuous-Time Analysis of Smoothed Matrix-Polar Spectral Gradient Flows for Muon-Type Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01911)
*Jinlin Liu,Song Chen,Jiaxu Liu,Chao Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究矩阵值无约束优化的平滑矩阵极谱梯度流，通过平滑谱势能引入谱反馈律，建立该反馈的规律性、单调性、有界性和耗散性，提出平滑谱梯度流并证明其适定性和全局收敛性，同时分析动量增强系统及局部下降率优势条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的极坐标因子映射在秩亏矩阵处失去光滑性，且当奇异值趋近于零时变得病态，这给矩阵值优化的分析带来困难。作者旨在通过引入平滑谱反馈律来克服这一分析难题，从而为矩阵值优化提供更稳健的理论框架。

Method: (1) 引入由平滑谱势能生成的谱反馈律，并建立其正则性、单调性、有界性和耗散性；(2) 基于该反馈律提出平滑谱梯度流，证明适定性和全局收敛性；(3) 在非凸、凸和Polyak-Lojasiewicz(PL)三种设定下推导收敛率结果；(4) 分析同一谱反馈律下动量增强系统的Lyapunov结构和收敛性；(5) 使用基于Hessian的二次模型，对平滑谱梯度方向与标准Frobenius梯度方向进行局部下降率比较，并给出可验证的归一化下降率优势条件。

Result: 建立了平滑谱梯度流的适定性和全局收敛性证明；在非凸、凸和PL设定下均获得了收敛率结果；动量增强系统在Lyapunov框架下得到收敛分析；局部下降率比较揭示了谱方向的优势取决于与梯度矩阵的一阶对齐程度以及Hessian诱导的方向曲率，并给出了可验证的归一化下降率优势条件。

Conclusion: 通过平滑谱势能构造的谱反馈律有效地解决了极坐标因子映射在秩亏矩阵处的光滑性缺陷，所提出的平滑谱梯度流在多种优化设定下均具有理论保障的收敛性质，且其局部下降性能可通过一阶对齐与方向曲率的可验证条件来评估。

Abstract: This paper studies smoothed matrix-polar spectral gradient flows for unconstrained matrix-valued optimization.The canonical polar-factor map loses smoothness at rank-deficient matrices and becomes ill-conditioned as singular values approach zero, creating analytical difficulties.We therefore introduce a spectral feedback law generated by a smooth spectral potential and establish the regularity, monotonicity, boundedness, and dissipation properties of the feedback.Based on this feedback law, we propose a smoothed spectral gradient flow and prove well-posedness and global convergence of the flow.We derive convergence-rate results for the spectral gradient flow in nonconvex, convex, and Polyak--Lojasiewicz (PL) settings and analyze the Lyapunov structure and convergence of a momentum-augmented system under the same spectral feedback law. Furthermore, we provide a local descent-rate comparison between the smoothed spectral-gradient direction and the standard Frobenius-gradient direction using a general Hessian-based quadratic model. This analysis yields a verifiable normalized descent-rate advantage condition, showing that the local benefit of the spectral direction depends on both first-order alignment with the gradient matrix and the directional curvature induced by the Hessian.

</details>


### [190] [Exact Prescribed-Time Control Based on Simple Harmonic Motion: Stability Analysis and Nonsingular Sliding Mode Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01952)
*Yi Ding,Bin Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了精确预设时间控制中的稳定性分析与非奇异滑模镇定问题。通过利用简谐运动的等时性，建立了精确预设时间控制框架，提出了新的Lyapunov分析方法，实现了标量系统的精确预设时间镇定，并扩展到滑模控制律，保证在外部扰动下几乎所有初始条件下都能精确预设时间收敛，且控制律在整个状态空间中非奇异、增益一致有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预设时间控制的稳定性分析和滑模镇定是控制领域的重要问题，现有方法在处理非零初始条件的精确预设时间收敛以及扰动存在下的鲁棒性方面存在局限，需要新的理论框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 论文利用简谐运动的等时性（isochronism）建立精确预设时间控制框架。首先提出一种用于精确预设时间控制的新型Lyapunov分析方法，实现标量系统的精确预设时间镇定；然后将该框架扩展为一种新颖的滑模控制律，该控制律在整个状态空间中非奇异且增益一致有界，保证了存在外部扰动时精确预设时间收敛。

Result: 理论上证明了对于任意非零初始条件，沉降时间恰好等于预设时间；所提出的滑模控制律在外部扰动存在下对几乎所有初始条件都能保证精确预设时间收敛，且控制律非奇异、增益一致有界。仿真结果验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过简谐运动等时性建立了一种全新的精确预设时间控制框架，兼顾了稳定性、鲁棒性和非奇异性，为精确预设时间控制理论研究提供了新的Lyapunov分析工具和滑模控制设计方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problems of stability analysis and nonsingular sliding mode stabilization for exact prescribed-time control. By exploiting the isochronism of simple harmonic motion, this paper establishes a novel exact prescribed-time control framework. First, a novel Lyapunov analysis method for exact prescribed-time control is proposed, based on which the exact prescribed-time stabilization of scalar systems is achieved. It is theoretically established that for arbitrary non-zero initial conditions, the settling time is exactly equal to the prescribed time. Next, the framework is extended to a novel sliding mode control law. It guarantees exact prescribed-time convergence for almost all initial conditions even in the presence of external disturbances. Notably, the proposed control law is nonsingular across the entire state space and maintains uniformly bounded gains. Finally, simulation results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [191] [Accelerating Evolutionary Strategy via Rao-Blackwellizing Realization of Uncertain Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02073)
*So Nakashima,Tetsuya J. Kobayashi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了表型加速进化策略（PAES），利用对已实现输入的可观测信息通过Rao-Blackwellization降低梯度估计方差，从而加速优化输入不确定性问题（OIU）中的优化过程，在连续优化到强化学习基准上均比传统进化策略收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入不确定性优化（OIU）问题中，目标函数的输入（而非目标函数本身）存在不确定性，常见于制造公差、物理系统控制、混合专家和强化学习等场景。现有方法通常只利用目标函数值而丢弃可观测的已实现输入信息，本文旨在探究这些被丢弃的信息能否加速优化过程。

Method: 通过Rao-Blackwellization在理论上证明利用已实现输入信息可以降低进化策略（ES）梯度估计器的方差，并基于该Rao-Blackwellized梯度估计器提出PAES（Phenotype-Accelerated Evolutionary Strategy），作为进化策略在OIU问题上的改进版本。

Result: 数值实验表明，PAES在从简单连续优化问题到强化学习基准任务中均比传统进化策略收敛更快。

Conclusion: 已实现输入的可观测信息确实能够通过方差缩减（Rao-Blackwellization）有效加速OIU问题中的进化策略优化过程，PAES是进化策略在输入不确定性场景下的有效改进。

Abstract: We investigate Optimization under Input Uncertainty (OIU), in which the input to the objective function, rather than the objective function itself, is subject to uncertainty. OIU appears in manufacturing processes with production tolerance, control of physical systems with actuation noise, Mixture of Experts, and Reinforcement Learning (RL). Most of the existing approaches solve OIU by using the value of the objective function but discard the information of the realized input, even though the realized input is observable in various applications. The question here is whether the discarded information of the realized input is useful to accelerate the optimization process. We affirmatively answer this question for Evolutionary Strategy (ES) by theoretically showing that the information of the realized input can reduce the variance of the gradient estimator via Rao-Blackwellization. Using the Rao-Blackwellized gradient estimator, we propose Phenotype-Accelerated Evolutionary Strategy (PAES), which is a refinement of ES for OIU. Numerical experiments show that PAES converges faster than the usual ES from simple continuous optimization problems to RL benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [A proximal gradient method with adaptive backtracking for weakly smooth multiobjective optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02201)
*Yuki Miyazaki,Masaru Ito,Shotaro Yagishita*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带自适应线搜索的近端梯度方法，用于解决目标函数为弱光滑（即梯度满足Hölder连续）的多目标优化问题。该方法无需预先知道弱光滑性相关参数，通过线搜索自适应地选择步长。非凸情形下的复杂度保证与相关工作兼容，且引入了更强的平稳性度量；凸情形下建立了优于非凸情形的新的复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标优化方法通常假设目标函数是光滑的（Lipschitz连续梯度），但在实际应用中许多问题只满足更弱的Hölder连续梯度条件。此外，现有方法通常需要预先知道光滑性参数，这在实践中难以获得。因此需要一种无需先验参数、能处理弱光滑目标函数的多目标优化算法。

Method: 提出一种近端梯度方法，结合自适应线搜索策略。线搜索机制能够根据弱光滑性自动确定合适的步长，使算法成为参数无关（parameter-free）的方法。算法同时考虑了多目标优化的特殊结构，并通过合适的平稳性度量来衡量收敛性。

Result: 非凸情形下的复杂度分析与已有相关工作兼容，且采用了更严格的平稳性度量（coercer stationarity measure）；凸情形下建立了新的复杂度结果，该结果优于非凸情形下的复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 该算法既能适应弱光滑目标函数，又无需先验参数，同时在不同凸性假设下提供了具有竞争力的复杂度保证，为多目标优化中弱光滑问题的求解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a proximal gradient method with adaptive linesearch for multiobjective optimization problems whose objective functions are weakly smooth, i.e., they have Hölder continuous gradients. The proposed method is parameter-free as we do not require prior knowledge of parameters related to the weak smoothness of the objective function; the proposed linesearch finds an appropriate step-size that adapts to the weak smoothness. The complexity guarantee analyzed in this paper for the non-convex case is compatible with related works and our algorithm accepts coercer stationarity measure compared to existing methods. We also establish a novel complexity result for the convex case which improves the one in non-convex case.

</details>


### [193] [Discrete-Time Adaptive Control in High Dimensions: Near Dimension-Free Performance via Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02256)
*Mohammad Boveiri,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高维离散时间非线性系统的自适应控制问题，针对具有可开发结构（如稀疏性、低秩、行随机或密度矩阵结构）的未知矩阵参数，提出一类基于镜像下降的自适应律，配合非欧几里得Polyak步长，在渐近状态收敛和遗憾界上仅依赖环境维度的对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高容量模型被用于实时控制问题，但高维未知参数的自适应控制在暂态性能上存在挑战。标准的欧几里得更新方案（如递归最小二乘和梯度下降）在高维环境下暂态性能随维度增加而恶化，即便真实参数具有低内在复杂度。因此需要一种能利用参数结构信息进行高效的在线自适应控制方法。

Method: 提出一类新颖的镜像下降型自适应控制律，配以非欧几里得Polyak型步长，利用参数结构（entrywise稀疏、组稀疏、低秩、行随机或密度矩阵）所诱导的几何特性。相对标称控制器定义遗憾准则来量化暂态性能，并建立渐近状态收敛性和遗憾界分析。

Result: 建立了所提更新律的渐近状态收敛性，推导出遗憾界对环境维度至多呈对数依赖；数值实验验证了所提方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 标准欧几里得更新方案对高维结构化参数控制问题不适用，而所提出的非欧几里得镜像下降自适应律能有效利用参数几何结构，在高维环境下实现优异的暂态性能和收敛保证。

Abstract: Motivated by the use of modern high-capacity models in real-time control problems, this paper studies the adaptive control of high-dimensional discrete-time nonlinear systems with an unknown matrix-valued parameter. We focus on regimes where the number of unknown parameter entries is large, but the parameter matrix possesses exploitable structure, such as entrywise sparsity, group sparsity, low rank, and row-stochastic or density-matrix structure. To quantify transient performance, we consider a regret criterion relative to a nominal controller with full knowledge of the true parameter. We show that standard Euclidean update schemes, including recursive least squares and gradient descent, are ill-suited to this setting: their transient performance deteriorates as the dimension increases, even when the true parameter has low intrinsic complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a novel class of mirror-descent-type adaptive laws equipped with a non-Euclidean Polyak-type step size that exploit the geometry induced by the parameter structure. For the proposed update laws, we establish asymptotic state convergence and derive regret bounds with at most logarithmic dependence on the ambient dimension. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [194] [Dynamic Traffic Allocation for Revenue Maximization on Creator Economy Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02293)
*Zhengli Wang,Franklin Lin Feng,Zhixi Wan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个连续时间动态优化模型，研究创作者经济平台在"向头部创作者分配流量以获取即时广告收入"与"扶持新兴创作者以建立粉丝基础"之间的战略权衡。模型揭示了"最有价值创作者优先"的最优流量分配策略，并在Bass扩散动态下展现出"条件性反转"策略。最优策略作为选择性守门人，既不像短视策略那样导致"赢家通吃"，也不像公平启发式那样导致"无差别增长"，并通过设定期望天花板防止过度投资。文章还发现简单启发式可导致高达25%的收入损失，并提出了一种接近最优的"粉丝增长调整"启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创作者经济平台面临根本性战略困境：流量分配应在短期广告收入（分配给明星创作者）与长期粉丝基础建设（扶持新兴创作者）之间如何权衡？现有研究缺乏对这一动态优化问题的系统分析，尤其是对异构创作者群体、双重收入流及生态结构影响的深入刻画。

Method: 采用连续时间动态优化模型，刻画平台在管理异构创作者（具有广告收入和粉丝直接贡献两种收入流）时的最优流量分配策略。在Bass扩散动态假设下，通过解析方法推导最优策略结构，并与短视策略和公平启发式策略进行对比分析。

Result: (1) 推导出"最有价值创作者优先"规则，该规则由前瞻性的激活集驱动；(2) 在Bass扩散下呈现"条件性反转"策略，平台会暂时优先落后创作者以利用口碑效应；(3) 最优策略作为选择性守门人，设置严格的能力阈值筛选创作者，并对成功入局者设定期望上限以控制过度投资；(4) 简单启发式策略可导致高达25%的收入损失，而提出的"粉丝增长调整"启发式通过利用观测到的增长动量实现接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 平台的最优流量分配策略应是有选择性的：通过能力阈值筛选创作者、通过粉丝上限约束成功者增长，而非短视式的"赢家通吃"或盲目的"公平分配"。这为创作者经济平台的运营提供了理论指导，同时也给出了实用的近似最优启发式算法。

Abstract: Creator economy platforms face a strategic dilemma: allocating traffic to established stars for immediate ad revenue versus nurturing emerging creators to build a follower base for future monetization. We develop a continuous-time dynamic optimization model to characterize the optimal traffic allocation policy for a platform managing heterogeneous creators with dual revenue streams (advertising and direct follower contributions). We characterize the optimal policy analytically, revealing a ``most-valuable-creator-first" rule driven by a forward-looking activation set. Under Bass diffusion dynamics, this policy exhibits a sophisticated ``conditional reversal" strategy, where the platform temporarily prioritizes lagging creators to capitalize on word-of-mouth effects. Regarding the ecosystem structure, we find the optimal policy acts as a selective gatekeeper. Unlike myopic policies that lead to a harsh ``winner-take-all" market, or naive fairness-driven heuristics that foster inefficient ``indiscriminate growth," the optimal policy imposes a strict capability threshold that screens which creators receive platform traffic. Furthermore, it enforces disciplined growth for successful entrants, capping their follower bases at an optimal ceiling to prevent over-investment. Finally, we demonstrate that simple heuristics can lead to significant revenue losses (up to 25\%) and propose a practical ``follower-growth adjusted" heuristic that achieves near-optimal performance by leveraging observed growth momentum.

</details>


### [195] [A Spectral Filtering Approach to Regret Analysis of Distributed Online Control for Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02375)
*Ting-Jui Chang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络化线性时不变（LTI）系统中的分布式在线控制问题，在对抗性扰动和时变凸成本下，将集中式Online Spectral Control框架扩展到分布式场景，提出基于谱参数化的分布式在线梯度下降算法，并建立了O(√T·poly(log T)/γ³)的次线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有在线控制研究主要集中于集中式设置，但在实际多智能体系统（如智能电网、机器人网络）中，每个子系统只能访问局部信息并通过邻居通信协作。如何在没有中央协调器的情况下，让每个智能体仅凭局部观测和通信，与最优的集中式线性策略竞争，是一个尚未充分解决的关键问题。

Method: 将Online Spectral Control框架从集中式推广到分布式设置。每个智能体通过将过去扰动与Hankel矩阵的前导特征向量卷积来构建谱控制器，控制器参数则通过基于局部替代成本的分布式在线梯度下降步骤进行更新，从而在保持次优性分析的同时实现去中心化控制。

Result: 在标准假设下，建立了与集中式基线（最优中枢线性策略）竞争的次线性遗憾界O(√T·poly(log T)/γ³)，其中T为时间范围，γ为稳定裕度，遗憾界同时刻画了网络规模与连通性的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在分布式在线控制场景中，通过谱参数化和分布式梯度下降即可实现与集中式最优策略竞争的性能，遗憾界次线性收敛，且显式体现了网络拓扑结构对算法性能的影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the distributed online control problem over a network of linear time-invariant (LTI) systems in the presence of adversarial disturbances and time-varying convex costs. The network cost is characterized by the summation of local cost functions, where each local function is sequentially revealed only to the corresponding agent. The goal of each agent is to generate a control sequence, using only local observations and neighbor communication, that competes with the best {\it centralized} linear policy in hindsight. We extend the recently proposed Online Spectral Control framework from the centralized setting to the distributed setting. In particular, each agent applies a spectral controller obtained by convolving past disturbances with the leading eigenvectors of a Hankel matrix, while the controller parameters are updated through a distributed online gradient descent step over the local surrogate costs. We formulate this problem this problem as a {\it regret} minimization problem based on the spectral parameterization, and under standard assumptions, we establish a sublinear regret bound of $O(\frac{\sqrt{T}\text{poly}(\log T)}{γ^3})$, where $T$ is the time horizon and $γ$ denotes the stability margin. The resulting bound also captures the dependence on the network size and connectivity.

</details>


### [196] [Timescale Separation Through the Lens of Operator Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02443)
*Guido Carnevale,Nicola Bastianello,Luca Schenato,Giuseppe Notarstefano,Ruggero Carli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了确定性及随机算子不动点迭代的时间尺度分离结果，从算子理论视角为互联动力系统的收敛性分析提供了显式可检验的参数界，并应用于反馈优化方案的收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间尺度分离是分析互联动力系统的有力工具，而算子理论为研究迭代方法（如优化、学习和控制中的算法）的收敛性提供了通用框架。然而两者之间尚未被系统地联系起来，特别是对于固定点迭代在时间尺度分离下的行为缺乏统一的算子理论分析。

Method: 本文提出了一个算子理论框架，将时间尺度分离结果推广到确定性和随机算子诱导的固定点迭代中。通过引入辅助系统来描述时间尺度参数趋于零时的极限行为，分别捕捉慢算子和快算子诱导的动力学，并给出可调参数在算子常数层面上的显式可检验界。

Result: 获得了以标准算子常数表达的时间尺度参数显式上界，证明了该框架在确定性和随机设置下的收敛性保证，并成功将其应用于反馈优化方案的收敛性证明。

Conclusion: 所提出的算子理论框架成功弥合了时间尺度分离与算子理论两个领域，提供了一种通用且可检验的方法来分析互联动力系统固定点迭代的收敛性，并展示了其在反馈优化等实际问题中的适用性。

Abstract: Timescale separation is a powerful tool for analyzing interconnected dynamical systems. Meanwhile, operator theory provides a general framework for studying the convergence of iterative methods formulated as fixed-point iterations, including algorithms arising in optimization, learning, and control. In this paper, we bridge these two areas by establishing timescale separation results for fixed-point iterations induced by both deterministic and stochastic operators. As customary in timescale separation, our results involve auxiliary systems that arise from the original interconnection in the limit as the timescale parameter tends to zero and separately capture the dynamics induced by the slow and fast operators. The proposed operator-theoretic framework yields explicit and readily checkable bounds on this tunable parameter, expressed in terms of standard operator constants. To illustrate the applicability of our results, we employ them to prove the convergence properties of a feedback optimization scheme in both deterministic and stochastic settings.

</details>


### [197] [Hidden Star-Convexity in Policy Optimization for Gain-Scheduled LQR: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02493)
*Shiva Shakeri,Péter Baranyi,Mehran Mesbahi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究增益调度线性二次调节（LQR）的策略优化问题，建立了一个精确的恒等式：当代价梯度使用极小值点的闭环协方差评估时，调度代价关于极小值点是星凸的。该恒等式在可行集上普遍成立，且收敛性由单一无量纲比率控制。当该比率满足阈值条件时，梯度下降在子水平区域内以显式速率线性收敛；而每个虚假驻点处该条件必然失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增益调度LQR的策略优化中，代价函数可能产生虚假局部极小值，现有的收敛性证明要么是局部的（仅在极小值附近成立），要么过于保守（覆盖范围太小）。需要建立更强、更全局的收敛性保证。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确的恒等式，将代价梯度与极小值点处的闭环协方差联系起来，从而证明调度代价在可行集上关于极小值点具有星凸性质。该性质对任意增益调度参数化均成立。基于此，引入单一无量纲比率，并利用该比率建立梯度下降的线性收敛条件和显式收敛速率。

Result: 梯度下降在比率满足阈值条件的整个子水平区域上以显式速率线性收敛到最优解；所有虚假驻点处该条件必然不成立。通过直接最大化该比率的实验表明，该阈值是景观的活跃边界。扩展版本包含完整证明和额外的数值研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为增益调度LQR的策略优化提供了精确且非保守的全局收敛性刻画，通过一个无量纲比率统一了收敛性的充分与必要条件，证明了星凸性质在可行集上全域成立。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for gain-scheduled linear quadratic regulation, where one schedule of gains, interpolated through fixed weighting functions, is optimized against a family of plants. The resulting cost can develop spurious local minima, and existing convergence certificates are either local or severely conservative. We establish an exact identity: when the gradient of the cost is evaluated with the minimizer's closed-loop covariances, the scheduled cost is star-convex about the minimizer. The identity holds on the entire feasible set, for any parametrization of the schedule. Convergence is governed by a single dimensionless ratio. Wherever the ratio satisfies a threshold condition, gradient descent converges linearly to the optimum on entire sublevel regions at an explicit rate; at every spurious stationary point the condition necessarily fails. Experiments that maximize the ratio directly show the threshold to be an active boundary of the landscape. This extended version contains the complete proofs and additional numerical studies omitted from the letter for space.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Uncertainty-Aware Simulation-Based Inference for Operations Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00019)
*Liang Guo,Lin Shaochong,Shen Zuo-Jun Max,Zhang Kun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不确定性感知、免训练的推理框架，通过短视向前瞻模拟量化下游预测不确定性，并利用重要性重采样动态选择候选步骤，从而提升LLM在运营研究（OR）数学建模任务中的连贯性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在OR任务中的正确性依赖于连贯的建模过程而非仅最终答案，但标准自回归生成采用短视策略，无法预判局部步骤能否有效扩展为全局一致的优化模型，导致局部看似合理的步骤可能传播为灾难性的下游公式或求解器代码错误。

Method: 提出免训练的推理框架：(1) 不更新模型参数；(2) 对中间候选步骤使用短前瞻模拟来量化下游预测不确定性或概率集中度；(3) 通过重要性重采样动态选择更可能产生连贯数学公式的候选。

Result: 在NL4OPT、MAMO和IndustryOR等多个OR基准上，该框架一致优于标准基线和低温基线，建立了高效、免训练的可信OR公式生成范式。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过不确定性感知的前瞻评估和重要性重采样，可以在不进行模型训练的情况下显著提升LLM在OR数学建模任务上的可靠性，为免训练推理范式提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) for operations research (OR) tasks remains challenging because correctness depends on a coherent modeling process, not merely a correct final answer. Standard autoregressive generation operates on a myopic policy, which sometimes fails to anticipate whether a partial formulation can be validly extended into a globally consistent optimization model. Consequently, locally plausible steps may propagate into catastrophic downstream formulation or solver code errors. To address this, we propose an uncertainty-aware, training-free inference framework for OR mathematical modeling. Without updating model parameters, our method evaluates intermediate candidate steps using short lookahead simulations to quantify downstream predictive uncertainty or probability concentration. Candidates that demonstrate a higher likelihood of yielding coherent mathematical formulations are then dynamically selected via importance resampling. Empirical evaluations across multiple OR benchmarks (including NL4OPT, MAMO, and IndustryOR) demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms both standard and low-temperature baselines, establishing an efficient, training-free paradigm for reliable OR formulation generation.

</details>


### [199] [Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00106)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可执行基准和一个预算感知的元路由器，用于从原始任务文本中组合异构操作（直接回答、分解请求、检索、执行代码、委派、验证等）。在保留测试集上学习策略达到100%成功率（静态流程93.5%），成本降低43%；但在词汇偏移挑战集上成功率降至75.9%（静态93.5%），揭示了词汇泛化而非路由执行是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统不仅需要决定产生什么答案，还需要决定哪些推理和执行操作应先于答案产生。现有路由工作大多孤立地选择模型端点、检索深度或工具，缺乏在真实可执行环境中对异构操作组合路由的系统性研究与基准测试。

Method: 构建包含216个训练、72个开发、108个保留测试和108个锁定的词汇偏移挑战任务的可执行基准，覆盖数据分析、冻结语料研究和文档处理三类任务；结果由机器在执行操作后自动检查。采用独立的正则化逻辑回归头预测操作概率，基于词和字符特征，在开发数据上进行温度缩放，并在路由成本和操作计数预算下进行贪婪组合。

Result: 在保留测试集上，学习的策略达到100%成功率，优于静态和固定流程的93.5%，成本比静态策略低43%；匹配的学习型一次性路由器仅达56.5%。在未触及的挑战集上，学习成功率降至75.9%，落后于静态路由的93.5%，但成本仍降低49%，且比一次性路由高出34.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 性能差距表明词汇泛化（而非路由执行）是主要限制。这些结果建立了可复现的测试平台和有边界的概念验证，但不代表实时LLM的性能表现。

Abstract: Agentic systems must decide not only what answer to produce, but which reasoning and execution operations should precede it. A controller may answer directly, decompose a request, retrieve evidence, execute code, delegate to a specialist, or verify an intermediate result. Existing routing work largely selects model endpoints, retrieval depth, or tools in isolation. We introduce an executable benchmark and a budget-aware meta-router that composes heterogeneous operations from raw task text. The benchmark contains 216 training, 72 development, 108 held-out test, and 108 locked lexical-shift challenge tasks across data analysis, frozen-corpus research, and document processing. Outcomes are machine checked after operations execute. Independent regularized logistic heads predict operation probabilities from word and character features, are temperature-scaled on development data, and are greedily composed under route-cost and action-count budgets. On the held-out test, the learned policy achieves 100% success versus 93.5% for strong static and fixed workflows, with 43% lower cost than the static policy; a matched learned one-shot router reaches 56.5%. On the untouched challenge split, learned success falls to 75.9% and trails static routing at 93.5%, while remaining 49% cheaper and exceeding one-shot routing by 34.3 points. The gap identifies lexical generalization, rather than route execution, as the principal limitation. These results establish a reproducible testbed and a bounded proof of concept, not evidence of live-LLM performance.

</details>


### [200] [MetaRoute-Bench: Evaluating Meta-Decision Policies for Agentic Workflow Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00107)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRoute-Bench，一个开放、可检查的基准框架，用于在共享执行模型下比较智能体系统的元决策策略（如直接回答、分解任务、调用工具、执行代码、委托专家、验证中间结果等）。基准包含180个合成任务档案、8种路由策略和30对随机种子，共43,200条追踪记录。实验表明任务感知的组合策略达到79.4%成功率，优于静态策略（76.7%）、一次性任务路由（67.4%）和直接回答（52.9%），但代价是更高的成本和延迟。消融实验显示限制组合操作和移除验证环节会带来最大性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统反复需要在直接回答、任务分解、工具调用、代码执行、委托专业系统、验证中间结果或失败恢复之间做出元决策，这些决策不仅影响任务成功率，还影响运营成本和延迟。然而这些元决策通常被嵌入编排框架中，仅通过聚合任务准确率来评估，缺乏系统化的方法论来比较不同的路由策略。

Method: 作者构建了MetaRoute-Bench框架，包含：(1) 180个覆盖数据分析、研究、文档处理领域的合成任务档案；(2) 八种路由策略（包括任务感知组合策略、工作负载特定静态策略、一次性任务路由、直接回答等）；(3) 30对随机种子；(4) 基于种子的离线执行模型来生成43,200条可复现的追踪记录。框架还包含消融实验（限制组合操作数量、移除验证环节）来分析各策略的性能贡献。

Result: 任务感知的组合策略达到79.4%成功率，相比静态策略（76.7%）有2.7个百分点提升（配对95%置信区间±2.0），但平均成本增加4.7%、延迟增加6.4%。一次性任务路由为67.4%，直接回答仅为52.9%。消融实验发现最大的性能损失出现在（1）路由组合被限制为单一操作时，（2）移除验证环节时。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是可复现的评估方法和路由策略权衡分析，而非生产环境有效性证据，因为结果来自种子化的离线执行模型而非实时部署。作者发布了任务生成、策略、追踪、测试和分析工件，以支持后续的实时系统验证。

Abstract: Agentic systems must repeatedly decide whether to answer directly, decompose a task, invoke a tool, execute code, delegate to a specialist, verify an intermediate result, or recover from failure. These meta-decisions affect not only task success but also operating cost and latency, yet they are often embedded inside an orchestration framework and evaluated only through aggregate task accuracy. We present MetaRoute-Bench, an open, inspectable framework for comparing meta-decision policies under a shared execution model. The initial benchmark contains 180 synthetic task profiles spanning data analysis, research, and document processing, eight routing policies, and 30 paired random seeds. Across 43,200 traces, a task-aware compositional policy achieves 79.4% success compared with 76.7% for a strong workload-specific static policy, 67.4% for one-shot task routing, and 52.9% for direct answering. Relative to the static policy, this is a 2.7 percentage-point improvement with paired 95% CI of plus or minus 2.0 points, at 4.7% higher mean cost and 6.4% higher latency. Ablations show the largest losses when route composition is restricted to one operation and when verification is removed. These results are generated by a seeded offline execution model rather than a live deployment; accordingly, the primary contribution is a reproducible evaluation method and an analysis of routing-policy tradeoffs, not evidence of production effectiveness. We release task generation, policies, traces, tests, and analysis artifacts to support live-system validation.

</details>


### [201] [Progressive$^2$: A Teacher-Student Progressive Co-Evolving Knowledge Distillation Method for Substantial Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00129)
*Tiancong Cheng,Ying Zhang,Zhiwen Yu,Yifang Yin,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种名为Progressive^2的新型知识蒸馏方法，通过"渐进增强的教师"与"渐进缩小的学生"的协同演化，解决了教师与学生能力差距过大时蒸馏性能严重下降的问题。教师侧按照从原始到丰富的语义渐进性逐步增加蒸馏层，并设计了基于Lipschitz连续性的多特征融合适配器提升训练稳定性；学生侧则逐步缩小网络规模，与教师进行迭代协同进化。该方法可灵活拆解使用，教师侧渐进策略可独立部署以平衡精度与训练效率，整体联合集成则进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏被广泛用于将大模型（教师）的知识迁移到小模型（学生），以压缩服务器端模型满足客户端用户的QoS需求。然而，当服务器能力与客户端需求之间存在巨大差距时，蒸馏性能会严重受损。现有方法无法有效应对这种能力鸿沟，因此需要一种能够缓解此问题的方案。

Method: Progressive^2框架包含两个关键渐进策略：(1) 教师侧渐进——不一次性蒸馏所有层，而是按照从原始到丰富的语义进展逐步选择额外层进行蒸馏，形成系统化的学习课程；同时设计教师侧多特征融合适配器提升训练稳定性，并以Lipschitz连续性理论框架提供理论支撑。(2) 学生侧渐进——不直接训练一个极小的模型，而是逐步缩小网络规模，与教师进行迭代式协同进化。

Result: Progressive^2作为一个灵活框架：教师侧渐进策略可独立部署，以实现精度与训练效率之间的最优平衡；教师与学生联合集成可获得进一步的总体性能提升，有效解决了能力差距导致的蒸馏性能退化问题。

Conclusion: Progressive^2通过教师与学生两侧双管齐下的渐进式策略，为能力差距悬殊场景下的知识蒸馏提供了一种系统性的解决方案，兼顾了训练稳定性、精度与效率的平衡，且具备良好的模块化灵活性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a widely utilized technique for transferring knowledge from a large model (the teacher) to a smaller model (the student). Owing to its flexibility and broad applicability, KD has been extensively applied in the compression of server-side models to meet the Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of client users. Despite significant advancements, the performance of distillation is substantially compromised when a large disparity exists between the capabilities of the server and the requirements of the client. To alleviate this problem, we propose a novel distillation approach, named Progressive$^2$, which operates through the combination of a progressively stronger teacher and a progressively smaller student. On the side of the teacher, rather than involving all layers simultaneously, we progressively select additional layers for distillation following a raw-to-rich semantic progression, establishing a systematic learning curriculum. Furthermore, we design a teacher-side multi-feature fusion adapter for the teacher to improve training stability, which is theoretically supported by the framework of Lipschitz continuity. On the side of the student, rather than directly training a tiny model, we gradually reduce the size of the network to facilitate an iterative co-evolution with the teacher. Progressive$^2$ serves as a flexible framework; the progressive strategy of the teacher can be deployed independently to achieve an optimal balance between accuracy and training efficiency, while the joint integration of the teacher and the student yields further improvements in overall performance.

</details>


### [202] [Rethinking Pretraining for Specialized Design Data: Evidence from the JONES-19 Cultural Design Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00135)
*Alexandros Haridis,Charles Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于JONES-19设计数据集（源自1857年《装饰语法》），比较了CNN在ImageNet预训练与从零训练两种策略下的判别性能，发现虽然域普适先验有助提升性能，但结合多裁剪局部采样的从零训练可有效弥补差距，表明在专门设计领域，精心策划的小规模高质量数据集可能比大规模通用预训练更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计与建筑档案以图形格式编码了领域专家的知识，为设计启发的机器学习挑战提供了关键测试平台，而这些挑战在典型计算机视觉基准中并不存在。作者旨在探索在专门设计领域中小规模高质数据集的训练策略价值。

Method: 基于JONES-19小规模图像数据集（源自The Grammar of Ornament），评估CNN在两种训练策略下的判别性能：（a）ImageNet预训练以获取域普适"视觉常识"；（b）在JONES-19设计数据上从零学习，并通过重复局部采样（多裁剪）进行增强。

Result: 域普适先验能改善判别性能，但从零结合多裁剪局部采样可有效恢复这些性能差距。对于高度结构化的设计数据，局部设计驱动的表示足以提供学习基础。

Conclusion: 在专门设计领域中，精心策划的小规模高质量数据集（能捕捉经验性与形式化设计原则）可能比优先进行大规模数据收集更有效、更富信息性，挑战了对大规模通用预训练的依赖。

Abstract: Design and architectural archives encode expert human knowledge in graphical formats, providing a critical testbed for design-inspired Machine Learning (ML) challenges absent with typical computer vision benchmarks. Building on JONES-19, a small-size image dataset based on The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1857), we evaluate the discriminative performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in two model training strategies: (a) ImageNet pretraining for domain-general "visual common sense," and (b) learning from scratch on the design data in JONES-19. We find that while domain-general priors improve discriminative performance, learning from scratch augmented with repeated local sampling (multi-crop) effectively recovers these gains. For highly structured design data, local design-driven representations provide sufficient foundation for learning, challenging a reliance on massive general-purpose pretraining. These findings suggest that in specialized design domains, careful curation of smaller high-quality datasets that capture empirical and formal design principles may prove more effective and informative on the nature of a particular design domain than prioritizing large-scale data collection.

</details>
